Guided by this analysis, GRACE uses reliability-gated alignment to correct composed orientations in parent-to-child order, leaving unconstrained components to the learned rotation predictor. The motion predictor and correction module are trained jointly with motion-derived supervision, using a frozen image encoder. Experiments under the TopoCap evaluation protocol show that the proposed framework improves motion recovery across different target topologies, including unseen structures. Reference-based cross-skeleton evaluation on a curated Mixamo benchmark and a user preference study further assess transfer to different target structures. A causal CAST-B variant achieves over 51 FPS on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, with timing covering image encoding and motion prediction. Code repository: https://github.com/fankewen/CAST.

</details>


### [68] [ScalLoc: Continuous Oblique Thermal UAV Geo-Localization with a 2D Satellite Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14866)
*Xiaoran Zhang,Yu Liu,Jinyu Liang,Huaxin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScalLoc，一种仅依赖地理参考2D卫星地图的连续斜视热红外无人机地理定位框架，通过"重建-精化-锚定-传播"流程实现尺度对齐的局部到地图定位，并构建Thermal-Oblique数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态热红外地理定位（TG）为GNSS拒止环境下的无人机提供光照鲁棒定位方案，但现有方法多针对近正射观测，或依赖地图侧高程先验，限制了其在仅有地理参考2D卫星地图条件下的连续斜视热红外定位应用。因此需要一种不依赖数字表面模型或预构建3D地图的连续斜视定位方案。

Method: ScalLoc采用尺度对齐的局部到地图定位框架，遵循"重建-精化-锚定-传播"设计：1）从热红外序列重建局部窗口几何；2）基于轨迹的位姿优化精化相机位姿；3）自适应地将每个窗口锚定到卫星地图；4）传播已验证状态以恢复帧级全局轨迹。同时构建Thermal-Oblique数据集，包含216条城乡昼夜轨迹、28,112帧带帧级标注的热红外图像。

Result: 大量实验表明，相比仅使用VGGT的配置，ScalLoc将平均定位误差从112.69 m降至26.97 m，降低76.07%，并在对比方法中取得最低的帧加权平均位置误差。结果证明仅使用轻量2D卫星地图即可实现连续斜视热红外地理定位，无需数字表面模型或预构建3D地图。

Conclusion: ScalLoc验证了在仅有地理参考2D卫星地图条件下进行连续斜视热红外无人机地理定位的可行性，无需DSM或预构建3D地图，并公开了代码与数据集。

Abstract: Cross-modal Thermal Geo-localization (TG) provides an illumination-robust positioning solution for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-denied environments. However, existing thermal geo-localization methods mainly focus on near-nadir observations or depend on map-side elevation priors, limiting their applicability to continuous oblique thermal localization with only geo-referenced 2D satellite maps. To address this problem, we propose ScalLoc, a scale-aligned local-to-map localization framework for continuous oblique thermal UAV geo-localization. ScalLoc follows a reconstruction--refinement--anchoring--propagation design: it reconstructs local window geometry from thermal sequences, refines camera poses using track-based pose optimization, adaptively anchors each window to the satellite map, and propagates validated states to recover frame-level global trajectories. To support systematic evaluation, we construct Thermal-Oblique, a real thermal UAV sequence dataset containing 216 urban and rural day--night trajectories and 28,112 thermal frames with frame-level annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScalLoc reduces the mean localization error from 112.69 m to 26.97 m compared with the VGGT-only configuration, corresponding to a 76.07% reduction, and achieves the lowest frame-weighted mean position error among the compared methods. These results demonstrate the feasibility of continuous oblique thermal geo-localization using only lightweight 2D satellite maps, without requiring digital surface models or preconstructed 3D maps. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/FloralHercules/ScalLoc.

</details>


### [69] [MORE: Learning Motion-Oriented Registers for Driving World Models and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14871)
*Yingdong Gu,Haoyu Wang,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Hao Han,Pengcheng Shi,Xianfei Li,Qinghua Tan,Wenlong Liao,Tao He,Jiayuan Li,Pai Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MORE框架，在视频预训练阶段直接学习面向运动的寄存器（Motion-Oriented REgisters）作为驾驶世界模型与规划的共享状态接口，取代继承自视觉编码器的密集特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型多继承预训练视觉编码器的密集特征作为世界状态接口，存在不匹配问题：视觉预训练产生信息丰富、空间密集的特征，但其面向运动条件世界建模与规划的适配被推迟到下游训练；同时密集网格使未来预测随分辨率提升而计算开销剧增。作者主张视频预训练应直接学习适合下游世界建模与规划的状态表示。

Method: MORE基于V-JEPA框架，在视频预训练中学习运动导向寄存器作为共享状态接口。方法将时间对齐的自车运动注入一小组寄存器，并将其作为掩码patch预测的唯一上下文，通过紧凑的状态瓶颈传递密集patch级监督；辅以分块寄存器掩码目标，在寄存器空间塑造预测结构。下游训练中，同一寄存器空间定义世界模型预测的未来状态，并为轨迹生成与评分提供共享场景记忆。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v2上，MORE的EPDMS较最优方法提升1.6，同时推理延迟仅为DriveLaW的1/10，效率更优。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在视频预训练阶段直接学习用于世界建模与规划的状态接口是有效的。

Abstract: By predicting future states, latent world models provide rich self-supervision for end-to-end driving, but often inherit dense features from pretrained visual encoders as their world-state interface. This creates a mismatch: visual pretraining produces information-rich, spatially dense features, but their adaptation for motion-conditioned world modeling and planning is deferred to downstream training. The dense grid also makes future prediction more computationally expensive as visual resolution increases. We therefore argue that video pretraining should directly learn state representations suited to downstream world modeling and planning, rather than relying on downstream training to adapt inherited visual features. We propose ***MORE***, a V-JEPA framework that learns **M**otion-**O**riented **RE**gisters during video pretraining as a shared state interface for driving world models and planning. MORE injects temporally aligned ego motion into a small set of registers and uses them as the sole context for masked patch prediction, channeling dense patch-level supervision through a compact state bottleneck. A complementary blockwise register-masking objective further shapes predictive structure in the register space. During downstream training, the same register space defines the future states predicted by the world model and provides shared scene memory for trajectory generation and scoring. On NAVSIM-v2, MORE outperforms the state of the art by 1.6 in EPDMS, while achieving superior efficiency with only 1/10 the inference latency of DriveLaW. These results support learning the state interface for world modeling and planning directly during video pretraining.

</details>


### [70] [Engram Neuron Inspired Adaptive Memorization for Lifelong Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14940)
*Suiyang Jiang,Bomin Wang,Hangqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文受生物记忆印迹神经元全生命周期启发，从梯度表示构建结构化记忆，用于调控参数更新与记忆管理，实现兼具稳定性与灵活性的终身学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工终身学习方法主要致力于缓解灾难性遗忘，但缺乏像生物系统那样自适应更新记忆的能力，因此需要一个能动态重组新旧经验、兼顾稳定性和灵活知识整合的学习者。

Method: 将印迹神经元的完整记忆生命周期整合进人工学习器，提出一个框架：从梯度表示构建结构化记忆，并利用该记忆调控参数更新、指导记忆管理。

Result: 在四个基准上进行综合评估，方法展现出良好的终身学习能力，在长任务序列上保持稳定性，并支持灵活适应。

Conclusion: 除性能提升外，该工作为鲁棒且灵活的终身学习提供了计算视角，与生物记忆系统中的机制相呼应。

Abstract: Lifelong learning enables artificial systems to adapt to changing environments. To achieve this, an effective artificial learner should dynamically reorganize past and new experiences, maintaining stability while enabling flexible knowledge integration. However, existing efforts primarily mitigate catastrophic forgetting, lacking the ability to adaptively update memory akin to biological systems. Here, by integrating the full memory life cycle of engram neurons into artificial learners, we propose a framework that constructs a structured memory from gradient representations, which regulates parameter updates and guides memory management. Through comprehensive evaluations on four benchmarks, our approach shows promising lifelong learning capability, maintaining stability over long task sequences and supporting flexible adaptation. Beyond the performance gains, our approach offers a computational perspective on robust and flexible lifelong learning, echoing mechanisms observed in biological memory systems.

</details>


### [71] [Text-Guided Medical Image Segmentation via Residual Aggregation and Dense Alignment Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14944)
*Jie Gui,Hang Tu,Yunfeng Dou,Wen Sha,Xiuquan Du,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RADAR参数高效框架，解耦并协同BiomedCLIP的医学语义锚定能力与MedSAM2的提示驱动掩码解码能力，通过LoRA实现低成本任务适配，仅0.74M可训练参数即在三个公开医学图像分割数据集上取得竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指定分割目标可为医学图像分割提供灵活的语义条件，但现有方法难以可靠地将高层文本语义落地为准确的像素级空间提示，尤其在低对比度病灶、模糊边界和视觉相似的解剖语境下表现不佳。

Method: RADAR显式解耦并协同BiomedCLIP的医学语义锚定能力与MedSAM2的提示驱动掩码解码能力，并用LoRA实现低成本任务适配。具体包含三个模块：(1) Guided Residual Depth Aggregation，在语义引导下自适应聚合跨层特征，缓解文本注入延迟与深层语义稀释；(2) Structured Dense Representation Alignment，利用冻结的BiomedCLIP构建裁剪级的目标、边界与上下文锚点，提升稠密特征的局部判别力与自动提示可靠性；(3) Multi-modal Prompt Generation，将跨模态语义响应转化为文本、点和掩码组合提示，供MedSAM2解码。

Result: 在三个公开医学图像分割数据集上，RADAR仅用0.74M可训练参数即达到具有竞争力的性能，验证了其在参数高效适配、语义落地和稠密表征增强方面的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RADAR通过解耦双模型能力并结合残差聚合与稠密对齐表示，在极低参数量下实现了可靠的文本引导医学图像分割，为参数高效的语义条件分割提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Specifying segmentation targets with natural language provides flexible semantic conditioning for medical image segmentation. However, existing methods still struggle to reliably ground high-level textual semantics into accurate pixel-level spatial prompts, especially under low-contrast lesions, ambiguous boundaries, and visually similar anatomical contexts. In this paper, we propose RADAR (Residual Aggregation and Dense Alignment Representations), a parameter-efficient framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. RADAR explicitly decouples and synergizes the medical semantic anchoring capability of BiomedCLIP with the prompt-driven mask decoding ability of MedSAM2, while enabling low-cost task adaptation via LoRA. Specifically, Guided Residual Depth Aggregation adaptively aggregates cross-layer features under semantic guidance to alleviate delayed textual injection and deep semantic dilution. Structured Dense Representation Alignment leverages a frozen BiomedCLIP to construct crop-level target, boundary, and context anchors, improving the local discriminability of dense features and the reliability of automatic prompts. Multi-modal Prompt Generation further converts cross-modal semantic responses into composite text, point, and mask prompts for MedSAM2 decoding. Experiments on three public medical image segmentation datasets show that RADAR achieves competitive performance with only 0.74M trainable parameters, demonstrating its effectiveness in parameter-efficient adaptation, semantic grounding, and dense representation enhancement. The source code is available at https://github.com/JGuiidev/RADAR.

</details>


### [72] [DSM-Free UAV Visual Localization via Latent-Depth Variable Projection and Structure-Adaptive Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14945)
*Yibin Ye,Xichao Teng,Shuo Chen,Xiaokai Song,Yuli Sun,Qifeng Yu,Zhang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent-Depth Variable Projection（LDVP）与Structure-Adaptive Coupling（SAC），实现无需DSM的UAV视觉定位，直接恢复度量水平位置，并在多样场景中提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV在GNSS拒止环境依赖视觉定位，常规方法需geo-tagged正射地图与DSM高程配合PnP求解位姿，但高精度且现势性好的DSM往往难以获取，因此需要无DSM的正射-透视几何（OPG）定位方案。

Method: LDVP将透视视线上的未知尺度视为latent depths，解析消去深度并在给定旋转下闭式求解水平相机中心，把非线性估计降维为仅旋转问题，从而直接恢复度量水平位置，且不假设平面场景；SAC利用单目深度估计推断相对场景结构，在匹配点未被单一平面主导时选LDVP，否则选Planar-PnP；并给出局部可观测性分析以支撑自适应策略。

Result: 大量实验表明LDVP持续优于现有基于OPG的无DSM方法；SAC在多样场景中进一步提升鲁棒性，并显著缩小与基于DSM定位方法之间的差距。代码将发布于https://github.com/UAV-AVL/LDVP-SAC。

Conclusion: 该工作通过LDVP直接求解度量水平位置并结合局部可观测性分析提出SAC自适应策略，为GNSS拒止下无DSM的UAV视觉定位提供了更准确且鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Visual localization enables unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to maintain absolute positioning in GNSS-denied environments. A common approach matches UAV images with geo-tagged orthographic maps and estimates camera pose using Perspective-n-Point (PnP) with elevations from Digital Surface Models (DSMs). However, accurate and up-to-date DSMs are often unavailable in practice. Orthographic-perspective geometry (OPG) enables DSM-free localization in scenes with varying elevations, yet existing methods typically estimate pose indirectly through intermediate matrix estimation and decomposition. To address this limitation, we propose Latent-Depth Variable Projection (LDVP), which treats the unknown scales along perspective viewing rays as latent depths. By analytically eliminating these depths and solving for the horizontal camera center in closed form given the rotation, LDVP reduces nonlinear estimation to a rotation-only problem, enabling direct recovery of metric horizontal position without assuming a planar scene. To improve localization robustness across different scene structures, we further introduce Structure-Adaptive Coupling (SAC), which uses relative scene structure inferred from monocular depth estimation to select LDVP when the matched points are not dominated by a single plane, and Planar-PnP otherwise. Our local observability analysis reveals how scene elevation variation affects the geometric constraints of OPG, providing a basis for this adaptive strategy. Extensive experiments show that LDVP consistently outperforms existing OPG-based DSM-free methods, while SAC further improves robustness across diverse scenes and substantially narrows the gap to DSM-based localization. Code will be released at https://github.com/UAV-AVL/LDVP-SAC.

</details>


### [73] [CatPortrait: Taming Video Generative Models for Portrait Animation via In-context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14957)
*Ziqi Ni,Zhou Fang,Shijian Wang,Rui Li,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CatPortrait 提出一种简洁有效的可控人像动画框架，将 3D 人脸引导以 in-context conditioning 方式注入输入空间，无需改动预训练视频生成骨干即可支持视频驱动与音频驱动的统一人像动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能合成高质量、时序连贯的视频，但用于可控人像动画时通常依赖架构改动或额外条件模块，导致复杂度上升、与预训练模型兼容性下降。

Method: 保留原始视频生成骨干，通过空间拼接将结构化 3D 人脸引导注入输入空间，实现 in-context conditioning；在同一框架内统一支持视频驱动和音频驱动的人像动画。

Result: 大量实验表明，CatPortrait 在视频驱动和音频驱动两种设定下均取得有竞争力或更优的结果。

Conclusion: CatPortrait 以简单有效的方式实现可控人像动画，在保持预训练先验的同时兼具两种驱动模式的统一性。

Abstract: Recent video generative models have shown strong capability in synthesizing high-quality and temporally coherent videos. Yet, adapting them for controllable portrait animation remains nontrivial, as existing methods typically rely on architectural changes or extra conditioning modules, increasing complexity and reducing compatibility with pretrained models. We present CatPortrait, a simple and effective framework that enables controllable portrait animation via 3D face guidance as in-context conditioning. CatPortrait preserves the original video generation backbone and injects structured 3D facial guidance into the input space through spatial concatenation, allowing pretrained image-to-video models to animate portraits without sacrificing their pretrained priors. CatPortrait supports both video-driven and audio-driven portrait animation within a unified framework. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CatPortrait achieves competitive or superior results across both settings.

</details>


### [74] [AD-GS: Anatomical Density-guided 3D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Dental CBCT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14963)
*Zonglin Li,Kexian Xie,Feiran Li,Chu Zhou,Yixing Huang,Ruiyang Chen,Zhanyu Ma,Heng Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AD-GS框架，将3D高斯泼溅的稠密化建模为衰减感知的MCMC采样过程，并引入医学分割基础模型的语义正则，在稀疏视图牙科CBCT重建中实现了更优的结构保真度与边界保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图CBCT重建可降低辐射剂量与扫描负担，但因投影信息有限而高度欠约束。现有基于3DGS的CBCT重建方法依赖梯度驱动的稠密化策略，在稀疏采集下不稳定，易导致结构过度平滑。

Method: 提出AD-GS（解剖密度引导的3D高斯泼溅）框架：1）将高斯稠密化形式化为衰减感知的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样过程，把高斯基元视为潜在解剖衰减分布的采样，使表示能力自适应集中于高衰减区域；2）引入基于Segment Anything in Medical Images零样本特征的语义正则，通过语义先验强制多视图一致性。

Result: 在ToothFairy3牙科CBCT数据集上，AD-GS在稀疏视图设置下相比现有3DGS类方法取得更高的结构保真度与更好的边界保持效果；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将高斯稠密化重构为解剖衰减分布下的MCMC采样并结合医学语义先验，可有效缓解稀疏视图CBCT重建中的欠约束与过度平滑问题，提升牙颌面结构的重建质量。

Abstract: Sparse-view Cone-Beam Computed Tomography (CBCT) reconstruction is essential for reducing radiation dose and scan effort, but remains underconstrained due to limited projection information. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based CBCT reconstruction methods rely on gradient-driven densification, which becomes unstable under sparse acquisition and often leads to over-smoothed structures. We propose AD-GS, an Anatomical Density-guided 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that formulates Gaussian densification as an attenuation-aware Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling process. By interpreting Gaussians as samples from an underlying anatomical attenuation distribution, AD-GS adaptively concentrates representation capacity in high-attenuation regions, enabling sharper structural boundaries under sparse projections. To further constrain reconstruction, we incorporate a semantic regularization based on zero-shot features from Segment Anything in Medical Images, enforcing multi-view consistency through semantic priors. Experiments on the ToothFairy3 dental CBCT dataset demonstrate that AD-GS achieves superior structural fidelity and boundary preservation compared with existing 3DGS-based approaches under sparse-view settings within the dental and maxillofacial setting. Code is available at https://github.com/yulinjiaxue/AD-GS-BMVC2026.

</details>


### [75] [Metric-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Object Hallucination Reduction in LVLM-Based Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14976)
*Thanh Nguyen,Hee Suk Yoon,Tung Minh Luu,Eunseop Yoon,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种完全由NLP指标驱动的强化学习框架，通过物体一致性得分、KL正则化与轻量PPO变体，有效缓解LVLM图像描述中的物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在图像描述中仍易产生物体幻觉，即描述图中不存在的实体。现有基于数据增强监督训练和人类反馈强化学习的缓解方法高度依赖昂贵的人工参与，扩展性受限。

Method: 构建由NLP指标驱动的强化学习框架：提出Object Consistency Score（OCS），兼顾物体描述的正确性与覆盖度；引入KL正则化提供更密集的token级引导；设计轻量PPO变体，在冻结LVLM主干上微调adapter，以提升效率与稳定性。

Result: 在InstructBLIP基线上，COCO数据集幻觉减少最高达41%，Visual Genome达29%；结合BERTScore、METEOR等互补指标还可提升描述质量，验证了有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: LVLM可借助指标反馈有效自我纠正幻觉，为减少人工投入的可扩展强化学习框架提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have excelled in joint visual and language understanding, particularly in image captioning. However, LVLMs remain prone to object hallucination, describing non-existent entities in the given image. Previous remedies, including dataset-enhanced supervised training and reinforcement learning from human feedback, show promise but rely heavily on costly human involvement, limiting scalability. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning framework to mitigate object hallucination in LVLM-based image captioning, driven entirely by natural language processing (NLP) metrics. While optimizing reinforcement learning solely on NLP metrics offers scalability, it also introduces pitfalls: reward hacking, sparse feedback, and costly training. Our framework sidesteps these by introducing an Object Consistency Score (OCS) capturing both correctness and coverage of objects, incorporating KL-regularization for denser token-level guidance, and designing a lightweight PPO variant that fine-tunes adapters atop a frozen LVLM backbone, achieving efficiency and stability. Extensive experiments on the InstructBLIP baseline demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our framework, achieving a substantial hallucination reduction of up to 41\% on the COCO dataset and 29\% on the Visual Genome. Furthermore, the framework can enhance caption quality by incorporating complementary evaluation metrics such as BERTScore and METEOR. These results reveal a broader insight in multimodal learning: LVLMs can effectively self-correct hallucinations using metric feedback, motivating deeper exploration into scalable reinforcement frameworks that significantly reduce human effort.

</details>


### [76] [Retraining-Free Depth Pruning of Vision Transformers: A Cross Domain Study of Layer Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15005)
*Alessandro Viespoli,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将基于相似性的冗余分析方法扩展到视觉Transformer，通过测量层输入输出的余弦相似度识别低影响变换，发现DINOv2、SwinV2等深层视觉模型存在显著冗余，并据此进行剪枝与模型选择，在精度损失极小的前提下显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在NLP和CV中表现优异，但计算、内存和存储成本高，限制了部署。已有研究表明LLM存在大量冗余，视觉Transformer的冗余虽被研究过，但系统分析不足。

Method: 将基于相似性的冗余分析扩展到视觉模型：通过测量层输入与输出之间的余弦相似度，识别影响最小的变换；在多个Transformer架构上研究冗余模式；评估不同剪枝策略下推理速度与性能退化的权衡；并提出模型选择流水线，自动为给定数据集确定最优剪枝配置。

Result: 深层视觉Transformer（如DINOv2、SwinV2）与语言模型一样存在显著冗余。适度剪枝可实现大幅加速且精度影响有限：DINOv2可无需重训练移除20%的block，吞吐量提升1.24倍，每图像能耗降低20%，ImageNet-1K相对精度损失低于1%。所提模型选择流水线在各架构上平均减少FLOPs 13%–27%，平均精度变化在-0.3到+1.0个百分点之间。

Conclusion: 视觉Transformer存在可系统利用的冗余，基于相似性的剪枝与自动模型选择流水线能在保持甚至提升精度的同时降低计算量，为视觉模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have achieved strong results in natural language processing and computer vision, but their deployment is limited by high computational, memory and storage costs, motivating research into model compression techniques, such as pruning, while preserving performance. Prior work has shown substantial redundancy in large language models (LLMs), and redundancy in vision transformers has also been investigated, but it remains less systematically analyzed than in LLMs. In this work, we extend similarity-based redundancy analysis to vision models by measuring cosine similarity between layer inputs and outputs to identify transformations with minimal impact. We study redundancy patterns across several transformer architectures and find that deep vision transformers such as DINOv2 and SwinV2, like language models, exhibit significant redundancy. We then evaluate how this redundancy can be exploited to improve efficiency, analyzing the trade-off between inference speed and performance degradation under different pruning strategies. Our results show that moderate pruning can yield substantial acceleration with limited impact on accuracy, and that 20% of the DINOv2 blocks can be removed without any retraining, for a 1.24x throughput gain and a 20% lower energy per image at a relative accuracy loss below 1% on ImageNet-1K. We further propose a model selection pipeline that automatically identifies the best pruned configuration for a given dataset, reducing FLOPs by 13% to 27% on average across architectures with an average accuracy change between -0.3 and +1.0 points.

</details>


### [77] [CowCare: A Unified Visual Framework for Viewpoint-Robust Cattle Tracking and Spatiotemporal Heat-Stress Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15012)
*Xin Liu,Weiran Li,Minghao Zhu,Mina Han,Zhenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CowCare统一框架，将视角鲁棒跟踪与时空热应激识别结合，解决多相机奶牛监测中的身份碎片化与环境噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准畜牧中奶牛福利与热应激早期检测需要可靠的个体监测，但从原始多相机视频到自动诊断仍受限于跟踪与识别流程割裂、复杂物理环境，如密集相互遮挡与跨视角变化导致身份碎片化，以及相机振动等环境噪声和农业数据有限使呼吸急促、腹部起伏等细微热应激线索难以捕捉。

Method: CowCare包含跟踪模块CowTrack与识别网络TSDF-Net。CowTrack通过视角解耦聚合网络VDA-Net维持跨相机身份一致性：在严重遮挡下聚合局部纹理，并用视角解耦Transformer（VDT）抑制瞬态视角变化，同时引入因果在线自监督机制以适应动态农场场景。其输出的轨迹片段由TSDF-Net处理，其中时序稳定与聚合模块TSAM抑制高频抖动，扩散语义先验DSP利用冻结的预训练扩散语义增强细微行为信号并缓解过拟合。

Result: 在两个真实农场基准上验证：MVCow上CowTrack取得83.48% HOTA与87.72% IDF1的全局多目标跟踪性能；HSCow上TSDF-Net取得87.72% Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: CowCare是面向自动化牲畜健康监测的鲁棒端到端框架，有效衔接低级视觉特征与高级行为诊断。

Abstract: Reliable individual monitoring is essential for dairy cattle welfare and early heat stress detection in Precision Livestock Farming (PLF). However, translating raw multi-camera videos into automated diagnostics remains challenging due to disconnected tracking and recognition pipelines and complex physical environments. Dense mutual occlusions and cross-camera viewpoint variations fragment identities, while environmental noise, including camera vibrations, and limited agricultural data obscure subtle heat stress cues such as rapid breathing and flank undulations. To bridge low-level visual features and high-level behavioral diagnostics, we propose CowCare, a unified framework combining viewpoint-robust tracking with spatiotemporal heat stress recognition. Its tracking module, CowTrack, maintains cross-camera identity consistency through a View-Decoupled Aggregation Network (VDA-Net). Instead of relying on static global representations, VDA-Net aggregates localized textures under heavy occlusion and uses a Viewpoint Decoupling Transformer (VDT) to suppress transient viewpoint variations. CowTrack further incorporates a causal online self-supervision mechanism for continuous adaptation to dynamic farm scenes. The resulting tracklets are subsequently processed by TSDF-Net, a fine-grained video understanding network. Its Temporal Stabilization and Aggregation Module (TSAM) suppresses high-frequency jitter, while a Diffusion Semantic Prior (DSP) uses frozen pre-trained diffusion semantics to enhance subtle behavioral signals and mitigate overfitting. Extensive evaluations on two real-world farm benchmarks demonstrate the framework's effectiveness: CowTrack achieves 83.48% HOTA and 87.72% IDF1 for global multi-object tracking on MVCow, and TSDF-Net achieves 87.72% Top-1 accuracy on HSCow. These results establish CowCare as a robust end-to-end framework for automated livestock health monitoring.

</details>


### [78] [Appearance Compensation in Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15030)
*Jianghong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在发射-吸收体渲染中，再现所有外部RGB视图的体密度仍可能不正确：外观场可通过补偿被移除的辐射来隐藏密度误差。作者用共享空间外观场刻画残存的密度信息，给出球谐重拟合后的最优密度增益、固定有限角度下正增益的存在性，以及精确的SH2物理歧义构造（中心密度翻倍、相对L²误差>14.52%，但外部RGB不变）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是质疑"渲染准确即可证明密度正确"这一常见假设。体渲染中密度与外观（源辐射）存在耦合，外观补偿可能掩盖密度变化，因此需要从理论上界定在何种观测条件下密度可辨识，以及渲染精度与密度恢复之间的本质差距。

Method: 在发射-吸收渲染框架下，引入独立参数化、物理有界（非负、有界）的有效源。通过有限阶球谐（SH）源重拟合，研究密度-仅增益；使用耦合张量规范（tensor gauge）与物理局域包（localized packets）确定尖锐的极限非线性逆系数及其倒数。结合共享空间外观场、连续像素测量、有限相机设置与透射测量，分析密度敏感性；并用受控训练实验与实拍烟雾剖面提供机理证据。

Result: 第一，得到有限阶球谐源重拟合后的最优密度-仅增益，其倒数为尖锐极限非线性逆系数。第二，在一个固定密度下，对一组各向同性空间源的残差集，在每个固定有限角度阶上实现正增益；增大角度容量可隐藏固定非零密度误差，同时保持物理性与给定平滑界。第三，构造出精确物理SH2歧义：中心密度翻倍、相对密度L²误差超过14.52%，但每条外部RGB射线完全不变。完整类证书可在同一学习密度下区分SH0、SH1与SH2拟合；有限相机结果揭示空间容量的独立作用，透射测量可分离原本相同的RGB场。

Conclusion: 局域可辨识性与"无容量一致恢复模量"可共存，即不存在随角度容量一致有效的恢复下界。因此仅凭渲染准确无法认证密度：密度恢复还依赖外观容量、空间分辨率与测量设计。作者据此提出将连续结果连接到共享有限表示、连续像素测量与密度敏感采集，为可辨识性与采集设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Can a volume that reproduces every external RGB view still have an incorrect density? We study this question in emission-absorption rendering with an independently parameterized, physically bounded effective source. Appearance compensates a density change by replacing the radiance that it removes from the rest of each ray. We characterize the surviving density information with a shared spatial appearance field. Our first result is an optimal density-only gain after finite-degree spherical-harmonic source refitting. A coupled tensor gauge and physical localized packets identify the sharp limiting nonlinear inverse coefficient as its reciprocal. Our second result realizes positive gain at every fixed finite degree for a residual set of isotropic spatial sources at one fixed density. At the same references, growing angular capacity hides a fixed nonzero density error while retaining physicality and any prescribed finite set of smoothness bounds. Local identifiability and the absence of a capacity-uniform recovery modulus therefore coexist. Our third result constructs an exact physical SH2 ambiguity: central density doubles and relative density $L^2$ error exceeds $14.52\%$, yet every external RGB ray is unchanged. We connect these continuum results to shared finite representations, continuous pixel measurements and density-sensitive acquisition. Complete-class certificates separate SH0, SH1 and SH2 fits at one learned density. Finite-camera results expose the distinct role of spatial capacity, and transmission measurements separate otherwise identical RGB fields. Controlled training tests and captured-smoke profiles supply mechanism evidence with explicit observation scopes. The results show why rendering accuracy alone cannot certify density: recovery also depends on appearance capacity, spatial resolution and measurement design.

</details>


### [79] [What Really Works for Universal Multimodal Embeddings? A Controlled Study of Contrastive Training Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15035)
*Duo Li,Haojun Pan,Zuhao Yang,Xintong Wang,Chengfu Huo,Shijian Lu,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评测揭示多数对比学习策略在MMEB上仅带来边际收益甚至负收益，并给出失败模式诊断与协同训练配方，平均提升6.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习已成为多模态表示学习的主流范式，负样本选择、批构造、梯度调制等策略层出不穷，但在可控可复现条件下其真实有效性缺乏系统验证，因此需要回答"复杂对比策略是否稳定带来下游收益"这一根本问题。

Method: 在MMEB上构建统一基准框架，对代表性对比策略进行系统评测；并提出MLLM-as-a-Judge诊断框架，将top-1检索行为分解为标注正样本、假阴性（未标注的有效匹配）以及难/易负样本错误三类。

Result: 评测显示多数策略相对标准对齐基线仅有边际增益，部分甚至降低整体性能。诊断得到三点发现：(1) 梯度失真——对高相似度负样本加权会持续损害下游泛化；(2) 分布幻觉——正负相似度分布间隔变宽不一定带来更好的检索性能；(3) 协同训练——假阴性缓解或难批构造单独使用收益有限，而将难负样本挖掘、假阴性缓解与难批构造结合可达到最优，在Qwen2-VL-2B上于MMEB平均提升6.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作厘清了流行对比策略的实际作用边界，指出单一策略的局限与失败模式，并提供了可操作的通用多模态嵌入模型训练配方，强调多策略协同的重要性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become the standard paradigm for multimodal representation learning, aligning semantically matched pairs while separating mismatched ones in a shared embedding space. Although various contrastive training strategies have been proposed across negative selection, batch construction, and gradient modulation, their actual effectiveness under controlled, reproducible conditions remains poorly understood. This motivates a fundamental question: Do sophisticated contrastive strategies consistently bring meaningful downstream gains in multimodal embedding learning? To address this, we systematically evaluate representative strategies under a unified benchmarking framework on MMEB. Surprisingly, direct evaluations reveal that most existing strategies offer marginal gains over a standard aligned baseline, with several even degrading overall performance. To uncover the underlying failure modes, we introduce an MLLM-as-a-Judge diagnostic framework that dissects top-1 retrieval behaviors into labeled positives, false negatives (unlabeled valid matches), and hard/easy negative errors. Our systematic analysis yields three key insights: (1) Gradient distortion: Upweighting gradients from high-similarity negatives consistently hurts downstream generalization; (2) Distribution illusion: Wider separation between positive and negative similarity distributions does not necessarily translate to better retrieval performance; and (3) Synergistic training: While false-negative mitigation or hard-batch construction yields limited gains in isolation, integrating hard-negative mining, false-negative mitigation, and hard-batch construction achieves optimal performance, delivering an average improvement of 6.0% on MMEB with Qwen2-VL-2B. Overall, our findings demystify popular contrastive strategies and provide actionable recipes for training universal multimodal embedding models.

</details>


### [80] [Use Of Convolutional Neural Networks In Detection Of Branch Dieback Disease In Pequi Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15049)
*Ronilson Rodrigues Pinho,Elua Ramos Coutinho,Nilza de Lima Pereira Sales,Sildenir Aalves Ribeiro,Sergio Manuel Serra da Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较AlexNet、Inception、LeNet、VGG16与MobileNet五种卷积神经网络架构在检测pequi树梢枯病（seca-dos-ponteiros）中的诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pequi树的梢枯病严重威胁树木发育与果实产量，造成显著的经济与生态损失，亟需高效准确的诊断手段。本研究动机在于评估不同CNN架构在该病害识别任务中的适用性，以筛选最优模型。

Method: 采用基于Python的计算机视觉方法，使用Scikit-learn、NumPy、TensorFlow和Matplotlib处理与评估视觉数据；将AlexNet、Inception、LeNet、VGG16和MobileNet五种CNN模型应用于叶片、枝条和花穗图像，检测纹理与色泽变化等病理模式。

Result: 通过五种CNN架构的对比分析，识别出在区分健康与患病植株方面最有效的架构。（摘要未给出具体性能指标）

Conclusion: 该对比分析可为pequi树梢枯病的自动化诊断提供最优CNN架构选择依据。

Abstract: This study aims to evaluate and compare convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures, specifically AlexNet, Inception, LeNet, VGG16, and MobileNet, for diagnosing branch dieback (seca-dos-ponteiros) in pequi trees (Caryocar brasiliense). This disease represents a severe threat to tree development and fruit yield, causing substantial economic and ecological losses. The methodology implements computer vision techniques using Python libraries, including Scikit-learn, NumPy, TensorFlow, and Matplotlib, to process and evaluate visual data. The CNN models analyze images of leaves, branches, and flower spikes to detect pathological patterns, such as alterations in texture and coloration. Ultimately, this comparative analysis identifies the most effective architecture for accurately distinguishing healthy plants from diseased ones.

</details>


### [81] [Deep Learning-based Semantic Segmentation of Dental Radiographs: Comparative Evaluation of PSPNet, DeepLabv3, UNet, and UNet++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15061)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Nadeesha Herath Mudiyanselage Lihini Sangeetha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在统一实验条件下比较了 PSPNet、DeepLabV3、UNet 与 UNet++ 四种分割模型在牙科放射影像上的表现，发现 UNet++ 在 300×300 图像尺寸、batch size 为 16 时取得最佳分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科放射影像分割对疾病诊断与临床决策具有重要意义，但因图像低对比度、噪声、质量差异等因素而具有挑战性。既有研究多采用 CNN 类模型，但实验设置不统一，导致模型之间难以直接比较，因此需要公平、可控的对比实验。

Method: 使用 1269 张牙科放射影像，并在训练前进行一系列预处理。在统一、可控的实验设置下，比较 PSPNet、DeepLabV3、UNet、UNet++ 四种分割模型，考察图像尺寸与 batch size 等条件变化的影响；采用 DICE、IoU、accuracy、precision 以及训练与验证损失等指标进行评估。

Result: UNet++ 表现最佳，在图像尺寸 300×300、batch size 为 16 时取得最高 IoU 0.6719 与 DICE 0.7869，分割性能最优。研究还分析了不同架构下图像分辨率与 batch size 对模型性能和准确率的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了不同分割模型的优势，并表明基于深度学习的语义分割方法在提升自动化牙科放射影像分析方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient analysis of dental radiographs has become increasingly important for disease diagnosis and clinical decision making. In particular, dental radiographs segmentation plays a critical role as it is challenging due to several reasons, such as low contrast, noise in the images, variations in the image quality, etc. Previous studies have primarily addressed these challenges using convolutional neural networks (CNN)-based models. However, direct comparison of these segmentation models is still challenging due to the variations among the experimental settings. Therefore, we mainly compared four segmentation models, including Pyramid Scene Parsing Network (PSPNet), DeepLabV3, UNet, and UNet++, through a fair, controlled experimentation setting under varying conditions such as image sizes and batch sizes. For this, we used 1269 dental radiography images, and prior to training, we implemented a series of preprocessing techniques. To assess the models, we used several metrics, including Dice coefficient (DICE), Intersection over Union (IoU), accuracy, precision, training and validation losses. According to our results, UNet++ demonstrated the highest IoU score of 0.6719, and a DICE value of 0.7869, showing superior results in terms of segmentation, specifically when configured with an image size of 300x300 and a batch size of 16. In addition, we also explored the effect of image resolutions and batch sizes on model performance and accuracy under different architectures. Therefore, this study provides insights into the strengths of different segmentation models and the potential of deep learning-based semantic segmentation methods for improving automated dental radiograph analysis.

</details>


### [82] [Global Disaster Risk Assessment Using World Risk Index Data: A Geospatial and Visual Analytics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15064)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Nadeesha Herath Mudiyanselage,Lihini Sangeetha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用Python可视化工具（Matplotlib、Seaborn、Geopandas）分析2000—2025年世界风险指数（WRI）数据，识别出菲律宾、墨西哥、印度尼西亚和印度等高风险国家，为灾害预防提供可视化洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球面临资源枯竭、粮食安全、贫困与不平等、城市化、移民及环境问题等多重威胁，需要提前识别高风险区域以应对自然灾害。世界风险指数（WRI）可衡量各国对自然灾害及相关风险的脆弱性，但如何借助高效的可视化手段理解WRI数据仍具研究价值。

Method: 研究分析2000—2025年的WRI相关数据，采用数据可视化技术，使用Python的Matplotlib、Seaborn和Geopandas库进行数据处理与可视化呈现，WRI指标涵盖易感性、自然灾害脆弱性及应对机制等因素。

Result: 分析结果显示，菲律宾、墨西哥、印度尼西亚和印度的WRI指数值较高，表明这些国家需为自然灾害及相关情景做好准备。

Conclusion: 本研究通过数据可视化技术提供了理解自然灾害与威胁的洞察，有助于相关国家提前采取必要措施，提前防范风险。

Abstract: The world has seen several threats that include resource depletion, food security, poverty and inequality, difficulties associated with urbanization, migration, and environmental effects. The World Risk Index (WRI) is a metric that is used to measure the susceptibility of nations against natural disasters and related risks that is reported by the Humanitarian Data Exchange. The WRI is comprised of factors such as susceptibility, vulnerability to natural hazards, and coping mechanisms. The WRI index has allowed to recognize regions in advance in order to face threats and situations beforehand. This study was conducted by analyzing the data that was related to years from 2000 to 2025 using data visualization techniques. Python related libraries of Matplotlib, Seaborn, and Geopandas were used due to their efficiency and usability in analyzing WRI data. As per the analysis, it was identified that countries of Philippines, Mexico, Indonesia, and India were recorded with high WRI index values that showed these nations should be prepared for natural disasters and situations. Hence, this study has provided insights using data visualization techniques in understanding natural disasters and threats so that necessary measures can be taken

</details>


### [83] [BrainAid: Efficient Brain Tumor Detection Using Lightweight CNNs and Explainable AI for Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15077)
*Sai Sriram Gonthina,Ross Gore,Bikash Chandra Singh,Pronaya Bhattacharya,Sachin Shetty,Abhishek Phadke,Sandip Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrainAid，融合CNN与ViT对四类脑肿瘤MRI进行高精度分类，集成XAI提升可解释性，并部署于树莓派实现低资源实时诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤分类依赖专家人工判读MRI，标准不一且耗费资源，易导致诊断延迟；深度学习模型虽具潜力但计算需求大，难以在实时医疗场景中部署，因此亟需高效、易获取的诊断工具。

Method: BrainAid结合卷积神经网络（CNN）与视觉Transformer（ViT），对胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤及无肿瘤四类MRI扫描进行分类，并引入可解释AI（XAI）增强模型可解释性；模型部署于树莓派以验证低资源环境下的效率。

Result: 在公开数据集上，BrainAid达到98%的精确率、98%的F1分数和98%的准确率，性能优于VGG16、Inception-V3、ResNet-50等标准模型，并在树莓派上成功部署。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了实时、边缘端脑肿瘤分类的可行性，可为医疗专业人员提供快速诊断支持，提升资源有限医疗机构及欠发达地区的患者护理水平。

Abstract: A brain tumor is an unusual growth of cells in the brain that can be cancerous or not, affecting normal brain function and causing serious risk to health. Existing solutions for brain tumor classification, such as expert manual evaluations of magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans, are generally inconsistent as different professionals have different criteria and can be resource-intensive, leading to delayed diagnoses. Additionally, while deep learning models have demonstrated potential, they typically require significant computational resources, motivating the need for more efficient and accessible diagnostic tools that can be deployed in real-time healthcare settings. To address these limitations, this study presents BrainAid, which makes efficient use of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) for effective classification of four-class classification of glioma, meningioma, pituitary tumor, and no-tumor MRI scans, outperforming all the standard models such as VGG16, Inception-V3, ResNet-50 and so, while integrating Explainable AI (XAI) to improve interpretability. The BrainAid is trained to classify tumors with a high precision of 98%, achieving an F1 score of 98% and an accuracy of 98% using publicly available dataset. The model is deployed on a Raspberry Pi, demonstrating efficiency in low-resource environments. This system shows the feasibility of real-time, edge-based tumor classification, providing quick diagnostic support for medical professionals and enhancing patient care in resource-limited healthcare facilities and underserved areas.

</details>


### [84] [Region Aware Squeeze-and-Excitation Networks for Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15079)
*Jack Roberts,Praneeth Tummala,Kavi Sarna,Jeova Farias Sales Rocha Neto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用空间引导池化替代SE中的全局平均池化，实现区域感知的通道重标定，并给出BASE、SPASE、MAPSE、PLEASE四种模块，在分割与分类任务上普遍优于SE及空间-通道注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SE模块通过通道依赖建模提升卷积网络性能，但其全局平均池化将空间结构压缩为单个逐通道描述符，损失了空间信息，因而在图像分割等密集预测任务中效果受限。

Method: 将SE的全局池化推广为空间引导池化，使通道重标定具备区域感知能力。提出四个互补模块：BASE使用规则网格划分（Tiled Squeeze-and-Excite）；SPASE在SLIC超像素上池化，得到图像自适应区域；MAPSE从冻结的通用网络生成的语义图中获取区域；PLEASE通过轻量级分配网络端到端学习软区域分配。每个通道在每个区域获得一个门控，像素注意力反映其区域归属，从而连接通道注意力与空间注意力；PLEASE的软分配随前向传播自然产生，提供结构化空间可解释性而无需事后分析。各模块计算代价互补：部分几乎不增加参数，部分不增加可测延迟。

Result: 在CUB、DUTS、ECSSD的二值分割，PASCAL VOC 2012与ADE20K的语义分割，以及mini-ImageNet分类上，所提模块在多数基准上优于SE及相关空间-通道注意力变体，取得大多数最优结果。

Conclusion: 以空间引导池化泛化SE，可在保持低计算开销的同时实现区域感知的通道重标定，统一通道与空间注意力，并在多种密集预测与分类任务上取得更优性能，同时具备结构化空间可解释性。

Abstract: Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) modules improve convolutional networks by modeling channel-wise dependencies, but global average pooling collapses spatial structure into a single per-channel descriptor, limiting their effectiveness for dense prediction tasks such as image segmentation. We generalize SE by replacing global pooling with spatially guided pooling, enabling region-aware channel recalibration through four modules with complementary cost profiles. BASE formalizes Tiled Squeeze-and-Excite using a regular grid; SPASE pools over SLIC superpixels for image-adaptive regions; MAPSE obtains regions from semantic maps produced by a frozen, general-purpose network; and PLEASE learns soft region assignments end-to-end through a lightweight assignment network. Each channel receives one gate per region, so attention at each pixel reflects its region membership, bridging channel and spatial attention. PLEASE also produces its soft assignments as a byproduct of the forward pass, providing structured spatial interpretability without post-hoc analysis. The modules offer complementary computational tradeoffs: some add essentially no parameters, while others add no measurable latency. Across binary segmentation on CUB, DUTS, and ECSSD, semantic segmentation on PASCAL VOC 2012 and ADE20K, and classification on mini-ImageNet, our modules outperform SE and related spatial-channel attention variants on most benchmarks, achieving the majority of best results.

</details>


### [85] [VLCQ: Post-training quantization for deep neural networks using variable length coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15092)
*Reem Abdel-Salam,Ahmed H. Abdel-Gawad,Amr G. Wassal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLCQ——一种无需原始数据与微调的后训练量化方法，通过可变长编码放松定点编码约束，在更低比特下实现更高压缩率且保持甚至提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能后训练量化方法多依赖INT8定点整数，当比特宽度进一步降低时性能显著下降；在无法访问训练数据、不能微调全模型的约束下，亟需一种能在低比特下兼顾压缩率与精度的量化编码方案。

Method: VLCQ采用可变长编码，放松传统定点编码的约束，从而更灵活地探索量化空间；全程不依赖训练数据，也无需模型微调。方法覆盖per-channel与per-tensor两种量化设置。

Result: 在图像分类、分割、目标检测任务上，VLCQ相比SOTA后训练量化方法提升了压缩率与精度。per-channel设置下，在多数模型上超过FP32精度与PWLQ，相比FP32最高6倍压缩，相比PWLQ最高1.7倍；在优先压缩、容忍有限精度损失时，相比FP32最高12.25倍压缩且性能下降小于4%。per-tensor设置下与DFQ相当，并在不同任务和模型上提供更大的低比特灵活性。

Conclusion: 可变长编码可有效打破定点量化在低比特下的性能瓶颈，使后训练量化在无数据、无微调条件下同时获得更高压缩率与更好精度，为资源受限设备部署提供了更灵活的量化方案。

Abstract: Quantization plays a crucial role in efficiently deploying deep learning models on resource-constrained devices. Post-training quantization does not require access to the original dataset or retraining the full model. Current methods that achieve high performance close to baseline results typically require INT8 fixed-point integers. However, achieving higher model compression with lower bit-widths often leads to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose VLCQ, a post-training quantization method that relaxes the fixed-point encoding constraint, enabling more flexible exploration of quantization techniques through variable-length encoding. This approach achieves competitive or improved performance while reducing bit-widths, without requiring access to training data or model fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on image classification, segmentation, and object detection demonstrate that VLCQ improves model compression and accuracy compared with state-of-the-art post-training quantization approaches. For per-channel quantization, VLCQ surpasses FP32 accuracy and Piece-Wise Linear Quantization (PWLQ) on most evaluated models, achieving up to 6x compression compared with FP32 and up to 1.7x compared with PWLQ. When prioritizing compression with limited accuracy loss, VLCQ achieves up to 12.25x compression compared with FP32 with less than 4% performance degradation. For per-tensor quantization, VLCQ is competitive with Data-Free Quantization (DFQ), while providing greater flexibility to achieve lower bit rates across different tasks and models.

</details>


### [86] [TIDE: Trajectory-Informed Skill Distillation for Efficient Text-to-Video Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15098)
*Chengxing Zhou,Yuliang Huang,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIDE 将昂贵的迭代式提示优化搜索蒸馏为一个可解释、可复用的提示重写 Skill，单次 LLM 调用即可生成正负提示，仅需一次视频生成即可达到搜索式方法的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频模型对提示的具体性高度敏感，但现有迭代提示优化需在推理时反复生成视频并用视觉语言模型评估，成本高昂。作者希望将这一昂贵搜索过程蒸馏为可解释、可复用的技能，避免测试时视觉搜索与骨干微调。

Method: TIDE 将提示重写建模为一组以源提示为条件的规则模块，每个模块针对一个独立语义维度。蒸馏完成后，冻结的 Skill 会自动激活相关规则，在单次 LLM 调用中同时生成正向与负向提示，随后只进行一次 T2V 生成，无需测试时视觉搜索或骨干微调。整个 Skill 仅用 100 条提示、14 小时进行演化。

Result: TIDE 的提示优化效果与计算昂贵的搜索式方法相当；迁移到未见过的视频生成模型时，其表现优于专为该模型设计或训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 提示优化可被蒸馏为轻量、可解释且可复用的重写技能，在低数据与低算力下逼近搜索式方法，并具备良好的跨模型迁移能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models are highly sensitive to prompt specificity, while iterative prompt optimization typically improves generation quality only through repeated video generation and vision-language model evaluation at inference time. We propose TIDE, a framework that distills this costly search process into an interpretable and reusable prompt-rewriting Skill. TIDE formulates prompt rewriting as a set of source-conditioned rule modules, each targeting a distinct semantic dimension. Once distilled, the frozen Skill activates the relevant rules and generates both positive and negative prompts in a single LLM call, followed by only one T2V generation, without test-time visual search or backbone fine-tuning. Evolved using only 100 prompts over 14 hours, TIDE achieves prompt-optimization performance comparable to computationally expensive search-based methods. Moreover, when transferred to previously unseen video-generation models, TIDE outperforms existing approaches specifically designed or trained for those models. Code is available at https://github.com/xiningin/TIDE_T2V.

</details>


### [87] [Drive3R: Block-wise Streaming Geometric Foundation Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15112)
*Tianyi Yang,Haojun Yang,Jipeng Xiao,Xuesong Chen,Shaoshuai Shi,Li Jiang,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Drive3R是一种块级流式几何基础模型，通过块级因果注意力与置信度感知重锚定，实现长时域驾驶序列的高效多视角3D重建，并在nuScenes、KITTI、Waymo上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真闭环仿真依赖稠密多视角3D重建，但现有前馈几何基础模型难以应用于连续长时域驾驶序列：全局注意力具有二次复杂度且受GPU显存限制；因果流式方法存在累积尺度漂移与轨迹偏移；"分块后对齐"启发式方法割裂全局上下文并导致轨迹不连续。

Method: 提出Drive3R块级流式几何基础模型：1）块级因果注意力（block-wise causal attention），在缓解全双向注意力可扩展性瓶颈的同时避免纯因果流式丢失局部未来上下文；2）置信度感知重锚定（confidence-aware re-anchoring），检测不可靠的流式状态，重新初始化KV缓存，并通过SE(3)对齐拼接刷新后的片段，以缓解长程漂移。

Result: 在nuScenes、KITTI、Waymo上达到新的SOTA。在nuScenes上相较DVGT-2基线，AbsRel从0.1806降至0.1081，点云Accuracy从0.6750提升至0.5344，ATE从1.5812降至0.9092。

Conclusion: Drive3R通过块级因果注意力与置信度感知重锚定，兼顾可扩展性与局部上下文，有效抑制长程漂移和轨迹不连续，为长时域驾驶序列的高效多视角3D重建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity closed-loop simulation for autonomous driving increasingly relies on dense multi-view 3D reconstruction. While recent feed-forward geometric foundation models have revolutionized per-scene optimization, applying them to continuous, long-horizon driving sequences is computationally intractable due to GPU memory limits and the quadratic complexity of global attention. Existing scaling strategies are fundamentally limited, as causal streaming suffers from compounding scale drift and trajectory deviation, while rigid "chunk-then-align" heuristics fragment global context and produce trajectory discontinuities. To address these limitations, we introduce Drive3R, a block-wise streaming geometric foundation model. Its block-wise causal attention resolves the scalability bottleneck of fully bidirectional attention while avoiding the loss of local future context in purely causal streaming. Its confidence-aware re-anchoring further mitigates long-range drift by detecting unreliable streaming states, re-initializing the KV cache, and stitching refreshed segments through SE(3) alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Drive3R establishes a new state-of-the-art on nuScenes, KITTI, and Waymo. Specifically, on nuScenes, it improves over the recent DVGT-2 baseline from 0.1806 to 0.1081 in AbsRel, from 0.6750 to 0.5344 in point-cloud Accuracy, and from 1.5812 to 0.9092 in ATE.

</details>


### [88] [RIECNN: Real-Time Image Enhanced CNN for Traffic Sign Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15127)
*Reem Abdel-Salam,Rana Mostafa,Ahmed H. Abdel-Gawad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出实时图像增强CNN（RIE-CNN）用于交通标志识别，在GTSRB、BTSC、rMASTIF三个数据集上取得最高识别率，单张图像预处理与推理总耗时不超过1.3 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别对自动驾驶降低事故率、提升道路安全至关重要，但现有深度学习算法受环境影响显著且实时性能不足，因此需要一种兼顾高精度与实时性的方法。

Method: 提出实时图像增强卷积神经网络（RIE-CNN），采用实时、新颖的图像增强策略，可同时处理多个不同的交通标志数据集，相较现有最优架构在识别率和执行时间上均有改进；在GTSRB、BTSC和rMASTIF三个基准上开展实验。

Result: 在所有基准上取得最高识别率：GTSRB 99.75%、BTSC 99.25%、rMASTIF 99.55%；预处理与推理时间合计每张图像不超过1.3 ms，满足实时约束；并对亮度与对比度变化等环境挑战表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RIE-CNN在保持实时性能的同时实现了显著高精度，并验证了其在亮度、对比度变化等交通标志识别挑战下的鲁棒性，适用于自动驾驶场景。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition plays a crucial role in the development of autonomous cars to reduce the accident rate and promote road safety. It has been a necessity to address traffic signs that are affected significantly by the environment as well as poor real-time performance for deep-learning state-of-the-art algorithms. In this paper, we introduce Real-Time Image Enhanced CNN ({\our}) for Traffic Sign Recognition. {\our} is a real-time, novel approach that tackles multiple, diverse traffic sign datasets, and out-performs the state-of-the-art architectures in terms of recognition rate and execution time. Experiments are conducted using the German Traffic Sign Benchmark (GTSRB), the Belgium Traffic Sign Classification (BTSC), and the Croatian Traffic Sign (rMASTIF) benchmark. Experimental results show that our approach has achieved the highest recognition rate for all Benchmarks, achieving a recognition accuracy of 99.75\% for GTSRB, 99.25\% for BTSC and 99.55\% for rMASTIF. In terms of latency and meeting the real-time constraint, the pre-processing time and inference time together do not exceed 1.3 ms per image. Not only have our proposed approach achieved remarkably high accuracy with real-time performance, but it also demonstrated robustness against traffic sign recognition challenges such as brightness and contrast variations in the environment.

</details>


### [89] [TRIG: Toward Measuring Texture Realism in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15164)
*Zhiwei Xu,Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu,Liang Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIG，一种针对生成图像纹理真实性的评价指标，其骨干网络采用在纹理数据集上预训练的GAN判别器，并微调一个合理性头以识别局部纹理退化，在特征上计算真实与生成图像集合的分布距离，与人类真实感评分的吻合度优于基线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽进步迅速，但部分输出仍呈现不真实的纹理特征，且这些差异难以度量。常用评价指标的骨干网络多基于其他目标（如图像分类或图文对齐）训练，这类目标通常鼓励对承载纹理的低层变化保持不变性，导致特征主要编码语义与全局结构，因而无法显式捕捉纹理层面的差异。因此需要一种专门面向纹理真实性的度量方法。

Method: TRIG的骨干网络不是分类或图文对齐模型，而是在纹理数据集上预训练的生成对抗网络判别器，从而为自然纹理的局部统计提供学习到的先验。在此基础上微调一个合理性头（plausibility head），用于识别局部纹理退化，同时将自然的失焦与传感器噪声保留为正例。随后在该骨干网络的特征上，计算真实参考图像集合与生成图像集合之间的标准分布距离。

Result: 将TRIG应用于多个生成模型，结果显示其与人类真实感评分的吻合程度优于基线指标。

Conclusion: 以纹理预训练GAN判别器作为骨干并结合合理性头微调，可有效捕捉纹理层面的真实性差异，使评价指标更贴近人类的真实感判断，为生成图像质量评估提供了专门的纹理真实度视角。

Abstract: This paper studies the backbone used by metrics for evaluating the quality of generated images. Although generative models have advanced rapidly, some outputs still exhibit unrealistic texture characteristics, and these differences are difficult to measure. Widely used evaluation metrics provide limited insight into texture realism, since they often rely on a backbone trained for another objective, such as image classification or image-text alignment. Such objectives typically encourage invariance to low-level variations that carry texture, so the resulting features primarily encode semantic and global structure. As a result, texture-specific discrepancies may not be explicitly captured. We propose TRIG, a metric directed at the texture realism of generated images. Its backbone is instead the discriminator of a generative adversarial network pre-trained on a texture dataset, which provides a learned prior for the local statistics of natural textures. A plausibility head is fine-tuned to identify local texture degradations, while preserving natural defocus and sensor noise as positive cases. On the features of this backbone, we compute a standard distributional distance between a reference set of real images and a set of generated ones. We apply TRIG to several generative models and observe closer agreement with human realness ratings than baseline metrics.

</details>


### [90] [ExeWorldBench: Benchmarking Code-Based Executable 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15166)
*Xinge Peng,Zhen Fang,Ruibing Wang,Yongxuan Lv,Haodong Li,Guohui Zhang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yiting Lu,Lin Chen,Zehui Che,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ExeWorldBench，首个通过代码化 3D 世界构建来综合评估“世界理解”与“可执行实现”能力的基准，包含三个递进难度任务，实验揭示前沿模型在可执行世界建模上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从“复现世界外观”转向“构建能刻画世界运行方式的、可执行的世界”。这一转变需要可执行世界建模，它整合世界理解（推理世界底层结构、机械与因果原理）与可执行实现（将理解转化为行为由物理交互涌现的代码系统）两项核心能力。然而现有基准多聚焦物理推理、视觉保真度或行为正确性，评估呈碎片化，未能充分检验二者的整合。

Method: 提出 ExeWorldBench，通过代码化 3D 世界构建来综合评估世界理解与可执行实现。基准包含三个递进难度的任务：1）机制可执行性（Mechanism Executability）：从视觉观测出发实现单个机制；2）组合可执行性（Compositional Executability）：扩展到具有空间、机械与因果依赖的多阶段系统；3）意图驱动可执行世界合成（Intent-Driven Executable World Synthesis）：依据高层目标与物理要求构建可执行世界。

Result: 在前沿模型上开展的大量实验显示，现有模型在可执行世界建模方面存在显著局限。

Conclusion: ExeWorldBench 填补了整合式评估的空白，为发展更具物理基础、因果一致的世界构建系统提供了方向性洞见。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly evolving beyond reproducing what a world looks like toward constructing executable worlds that capture how a world works. This shift calls for executable world modeling, which integrates two core capabilities: world understanding, reasoning about the underlying structural, mechanical, and causal principles of a world, and executable realization, translating that understanding into a code-based system whose behavior emerges from physical interactions. However, existing benchmarks provide only fragmented evaluations centered on physical reasoning, visual fidelity, or behavioral correctness, leaving the integration of these capabilities insufficiently assessed. To bridge this gap, we introduce ExeWorldBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark to comprehensively evaluate world understanding and executable realization through code-based 3D world construction. ExeWorldBench comprises three progressively challenging tasks: Mechanism Executability, which evaluates the realization of individual mechanisms from visual observations; Compositional Executability, which extends to multi-stage systems with spatial, mechanical, and causal dependencies; and Intent-Driven Executable World Synthesis, which requires constructing executable worlds from high-level goals and physical requirements. Extensive experiments on frontier models reveal substantial limitations in executable world modeling, offering insights into the development of more physically grounded and causally coherent world construction systems.

</details>


### [91] [GeoBridge: Evolving Transferable Decoder Topologies for Geometric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15280)
*Rui Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoBridge进化框架，将解码器内部拓扑作为可搜索设计对象，通过保留阶段接口的拓扑搜索实现跨深度估计与高斯重建的复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度估计与高斯重建都需要多尺度特征处理，但使用不同的预测接口与训练目标；仅搜索原语配置无法改变变换的组织方式，因此需要探索解码器内部计算能否一次优化、跨系统复用。

Method: 1) 通过残差、顺序、并行组合可配置原语，使阶段内拓扑可搜索，保形组合保留固定多尺度骨架的阶段接口；2) 功能树编码支持阶段感知交叉与层次变异，梯度敏感度对齐代理实现免训练评估，多目标进化平衡代理、计算量与参数量；3) 在目标系统中实例化并训练所选拓扑，配合任务特定预测接口。

Result: 源搜索约需12 GPU小时；GeoBridgeNet-A3在NYU Depth V2与KITTI上，在共同编码器和训练协议下取得四个适配解码器基线中最佳深度精度；同一拓扑在RealEstate10K高斯新视角合成达到27.11 dB PSNR，其精炼表示支持LERF开放词汇分割。

Conclusion: 结果表明解码器拓扑可作为可复用设计对象，在所评估的几何视觉系统中实现跨任务迁移，代码与预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Depth estimation and Gaussian reconstruction share a need for multi-scale feature processing, yet use different prediction interfaces and training objectives. Whether a decoder's internal computation can be optimized once and reused across these systems remains a structural design question. Search over primitive configurations alone leaves the organization of transformations fixed. To address this gap, we propose GeoBridge, an evolutionary framework for transferable decoder topologies. First, GeoBridge makes stage-internal topology searchable through residual, sequential, and parallel composition of configurable primitives. Shape-preserving composition retains the stage interfaces of a fixed multi-scale scaffold. Second, a functional-tree encoding supports stage-aware crossover and hierarchical mutation, while a gradient sensitivity-alignment proxy evaluates candidates without training. Multi-objective evolution balances this proxy with computation and parameter cost. Third, selected topologies are instantiated and trained in target systems with task-specific prediction interfaces. A single source search takes approximately 12 GPU hours. GeoBridgeNet-A3 achieves the best depth accuracy among four adapted decoder baselines under a common encoder and training protocol on NYU Depth V2 and KITTI. The same topology reaches 27.11 dB PSNR in Gaussian novel-view synthesis on RealEstate10K, and its refined representations support open-vocabulary segmentation on LERF. These results support decoder topology as a reusable design object across the evaluated geometric vision systems. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/EMI-Group/GeoBridge-NAS.

</details>


### [92] [Protein Crystallization Segmentation and Classification Using Subordinate Color Channel in Fluorescence Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15286)
*Truong X. Tran,Marc L. Pusey,Ramazan S. Aygun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用发射光谱中峰值波长之外的次级（长波长）颜色通道强度进行分割，以提升痕量荧光标记（TFL）蛋白质结晶试验图像中晶体检测的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高通量与自动化系统中，蛋白质晶体检测的准确率至关重要。TFL方法虽能高亮晶体，但荧光染料的反射与发射并非总来自晶体区域，因此仅依赖发射光谱峰值波长的分析并不总是有效。

Method: 针对TFL蛋白质结晶试验图像，构建基于次级颜色通道（对应比发射光谱峰值更长波长）百分位强度的分割方法，并与使用主颜色通道的方案进行对比。

Result: 相比主颜色通道，次级颜色通道分割方法将"可能先导物或晶体误判为非晶体"的比例按分类器不同从9.71%降至2.02%；分类器准确率提升1.77%至5.53%；方法整体准确率约94%，且误判率低于1%。在Con A与AbIPPase两种蛋白的新湿实验中也验证了泛化性，误判率低于1%。

Conclusion: 使用次级颜色通道有助于TFL蛋白质试验图像的分类，可有效提升晶体检测准确率并降低漏检率。

Abstract: The accuracy of detecting protein crystals for fluorescence microscopy images is very critical for high throughput and automated systems. Although the trace fluorescent labeling method could highlight protein crystals, reflection and emission from the fluorescence dye is not always due to crystal regions. Therefore, the analysis of the peak wavelength in the emission spectra of a fluorophore may not always yield effective results. In this paper, we show that using the subordinate color intensity corresponding to longer wavelengths than the peak wavelength of the emission spectra could improve the accuracy of protein crystal detection. Hence, we have built a segmentation method based on the percentile intensity of the subordinate color for trace fluorescently labeled (TFL'd) protein crystallization trial images. Compared to using the dominant color channel, our segmentation method on subordinate color channel was able to reduce the misclassification rate of likely-leads or crystals as non-crystals by the percentage of from 9.71% to 2.02% depending on the classifier. Similarly, the accuracy of classifiers were increased by the percentage of from 1.77% to 5.53%. Our method reached around 94% accuracy while keeping misclassification of likely-leads and crystals as non-crystals below 1%. Moreover, to evaluate the generalizability of our method, we have conducted new wet lab experiments on two proteins, Concanavalin A (Con A) and Ab inorganic pyrophosphate (AbIPPase), and the misclassification rate was below 1%. Our experiments show that using the subordinate channel may be more helpful for TFL'd protein trial image classification.

</details>


### [93] [ASM-6D: Fast Certified Pose-Shape Inference with Complete Correspondence Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15287)
*Ho Jin Choi,Minku Kim,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASM-6D 将生成式完整几何预测与基于共享对应关系的类别级位姿-形状推断相结合，通过解析消元将拟合问题降维为九变量的缩放旋转优化，在 ROPE 和 HouseCat6D 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级 6D 位姿与形状估计需要在仅有部分观测、没有精确实例模型的条件下恢复物体的位姿与几何，传统方法依赖显式的对应关系分配阶段，效率与精度受限。

Method: 输入 RGB-D 图像，生成多个完整的 3D 对应假设，其点身份与类别级主动形状模型（ASM）共享（包含被遮挡点）；每个假设直接拟合旋转、平移、度量尺度与实例形状，无需独立对应分配。通过解析消去平移与形状系数，将拟合简化为九变量的缩放旋转优化，支持多假设的实时批量评估。

Result: 在 ROPE 与 HouseCat6D 基准上达到 SOTA；消融实验证明生成式对应预测与主动形状变化的重要性；该表示还支持形状补全、形状相关位姿歧义、跟踪与机器人抓取，速率 6–38 Hz（取决于推理预算）。

Conclusion: 共享对应关系耦合生成式几何与显式位姿-形状推断的框架，在保持实时性的同时提升了类别级 6D 估计性能，并具备向下游任务扩展的能力。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose and shape estimation requires recovering an object's pose and geometry from partial observations without an exact instance model. We present ASM-6D, which couples generative complete-geometry prediction with explicit category-level pose-shape inference through shared correspondences. Given an RGB-D image, ASM-6D generates alternative complete 3D correspondence hypotheses whose point identities are shared with a category-level Active Shape Model (ASM), including occluded points. Each hypothesis is fitted directly for rotation, translation, metric scale, and instance shape without a separate correspondence-assignment stage. We analytically eliminate translation and shape coefficients, reducing fitting to a nine-variable scaled-rotation optimization problem that enables efficient batched evaluation of multiple hypotheses in real time. ASM-6D achieves state-of-the-art performance on ROPE and HouseCat6D benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate the importance of generative correspondence prediction and active shape variation. The representation also supports shape completion, shape-dependent pose ambiguity, tracking, and robotic grasping at 6-38 Hz, depending on the inference budget.

</details>


### [94] [HGA-Quant: Heterogeneity-Aware Quantization for AutoRegressive Visual Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15297)
*Xinmeng Liu,Xianglong Yan,Kaicheng Yang,Weisheng Dong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练 PTQ 框架 T-BGR + CAPS，显著提升 ARVG 模型低比特量化下的图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARVG 模型图像合成质量高，但计算与内存开销大，难以部署到资源受限的边缘设备；现有 PTQ 方法在低比特量化下图像质量严重下降。

Method: 提出免训练 PTQ 框架，包含 Tail-Aware Bounded-init Grid Refinement (T-BGR) 和 Coarse-to-Fine Activation Precision Scheduling (CAPS)。T-BGR 引入尾部感知重建目标用于权重量化器校准，对分布尾部系数赋予更高权重，在不增加额外参数或高精度分支的前提下保留细粒度信息；CAPS 联合考虑不同生成阶段的重要性与计算成本，进行由粗到细的激活精度调度，在给定比特预算下实现高效混合精度分配。

Result: 在多个 ARVG 骨干网络上，框架在激进低比特量化下持续提升鲁棒性；在 Infinity 上以有效 W4A5.6 激活精度达到接近全精度的图像生成质量，而现有方法在 W4A6 下仍存在明显质量退化或不稳定表现。

Conclusion: 所提免训练 PTQ 框架能有效缓解 ARVG 低比特量化的质量下降问题，在更低比特预算下实现优于现有方法的生成质量。

Abstract: Recently, AutoRegressive Visual Generation (ARVG) models have emerged as a competitive paradigm for high-quality image synthesis. However, their substantial computational and memory demands make them difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) provides a promising solution to reduce the computational and memory costs of ARVG models, yet existing methods often suffer from substantial image quality degradation under low-bit quantization. In this paper, we propose a training-free PTQ framework for ARVG, consisting of Tail-Aware Bounded-init Grid Refinement (T-BGR) and Coarse-to-Fine Activation Precision Scheduling (CAPS). T-BGR introduces a tail-aware reconstruction objective for weight quantizer calibration, assigning greater importance to distribution-tail coefficients and preserving fine-grained information without introducing additional parameters or high-precision branches. CAPS performs coarse-to-fine activation precision scheduling by jointly considering the importance and computational cost of different generation stages, enabling efficient mixed-precision allocation under a given bit budget. Extensive experiments on multiple ARVG backbones demonstrate that our framework consistently improves robustness under aggressive low-bit quantization. Notably, on Infinity, our framework achieves near full-precision image generation quality at an effective W4A5.6 activation precision, while existing methods at W4A6 still exhibit noticeable quality degradation or inconsistent performance. Code is available at https://github.com/lxm-lc/HGA-Quant.

</details>


### [95] [Channel Match: Calibrating Shape Latents for Few-Step 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15321)
*Di Yang,Zhihao Li,Yanhai Xiong,Yufei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出少步蒸馏框架加速原生3D生成，并提出Channel Match方法修复少步采样导致的隐空间分布漂移对PBR纹理合成的破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生3D生成器通过结构化3D隐变量与流式Transformer实现高质量资产生成，但高分辨率生成因迭代采样成本高昂而计算昂贵。此外，作者发现少步原生3D生成存在一个被忽视的失效模式。

Method: 提出基于连续自一致性训练的少步蒸馏框架，配合定制的JVP FlashAttention核与针对稀疏结构化3D隐变量的解析速度回归目标；引入自适应损失加权策略自动平衡各损失项；针对采样引起的隐分布漂移，提出Channel Match——一种免训练的自适应逐通道隐变量校准方法，在不修改生成器的前提下恢复隐分布。

Result: 在TRELLIS.2的1024^3分辨率设置上验证：两步模型实现4.5倍几何采样加速，几何质量与多步教师模型相当；Channel Match进一步恢复几何与外观生成之间的隐接口，提升渲染质量。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏可显著加速原生3D生成的几何采样且保持质量，而少步采样引发的隐空间分布漂移会破坏下游PBR纹理合成；通过免训练的Channel Match校准可有效修复该问题，整体提升渲染质量。

Abstract: Recent native 3D generators achieve high-quality asset creation by modeling structured 3D latents with flow-based transformers, but their high-resolution generation remains computationally expensive due to costly iterative sampling. In this work, we introduce a few-step distillation framework for native 3D generation, enabled by continuous self-consistency training with a custom JVP FlashAttention kernel and an analytic velocity regression objective for sparse structured 3D latents. To stabilize joint optimization, we further introduce an adaptive loss weighting strategy that automatically balances them without manual tuning. Beyond acceleration, we reveal a previously overlooked failure mode of few-step native 3D generation: sampling-induced latent distribution drift preserves geometric correctness but disrupts downstream PBR texture synthesis, causing texture artifacts on geometrically valid assets. To address this issue, we propose Channel Match, a training-free adaptive per-channel latent calibration method that restores the latent distribution without modifying either generator. We validate our approach on TRELLIS.2 at the $1024^3$ resolution setting. Our two-step model achieves $4.5\times$ faster geometry sampling while maintaining geometry quality comparable to the many-step teacher, and Channel Match further improves rendering quality by recovering the latent interface between geometry and appearance generation.

</details>


### [96] [TempoKV: Predictive KV Cache Quantization for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15324)
*Jiahui Hu,Weile Mo,Peiliang Cai,Jiacheng Liu,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Xiaobing Tu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 \method，一种免训练的自回归视频生成 KV 缓存预测式量化框架，基于键与值不同时间依赖的观测，仅以低精度存储残差，实现最高 6.71× 压缩且质量优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型支持长视频流式生成，但 KV 缓存快速增长带来显著显存开销。现有 KV 缓存量化方法主要面向 LLM，迁移到视频模型效果差，因为视频模型激活统计不同且具有强时空冗余。

Method: 作者观察到视频 KV 缓存随时间高度可预测且存在明显非对称性：键可由时间历史高度预测，值主要依赖当前键并仅需较短的前序值历史。据此，\method 按各自依赖分别预测键与值，仅以低精度存储残差。两个预测器均轻量，通过少量 BF16 样本上的线性回归一次性离线校准，并在不同提示与生成轮次间固定不变。

Result: 在 Self-Forcing、Causal Forcing 和 LongCat-Video-13B 上，\method 在相当或更高压缩比下生成质量持续优于现有 KV 缓存量化方法，最高实现 6.71× KV 缓存压缩，并具备最佳的整体质量—效率权衡。

Conclusion: \method 是免训练、即插即用的预测式 KV 缓存量化框架，充分利用视频 KV 缓存的非对称时间可预测性，有效缓解自回归视频生成显存瓶颈，代码将开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models enable streaming generation of long videos but suffer from significant memory overhead due to the rapidly growing KV cache. Existing KV cache quantization methods are primarily designed for LLMs and transfer poorly to video models, where activations exhibit different statistics and strong spatiotemporal redundancy. We observe that the video KV cache is largely predictable over time, with a clear asymmetry. Keys are highly predictable from their temporal history, while values depend primarily on the current key and need a short history of preceding values. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{\method}, a training-free predictive KV cache quantization framework for AR video generation. \method predicts keys and values according to their distinct dependencies and stores only the residuals at low precision. Both predictors are lightweight, calibrated once offline by linear regression on a small set of BF16 samples, and remain fixed across prompts and generation runs. Experiments on Self-Forcing, Causal Forcing, and LongCat-Video-13B show that \method consistently achieves higher generation quality than existing KV cache quantization methods at comparable or higher compression ratios, reaching up to $6.71\times$ KV cache compression with the best overall quality--efficiency tradeoff. Our code will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [97] [BaT-SAM: Introducing Inductive Bias into SAM via a Mixture of Convolutional Experts for Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15330)
*Sumiaya Jannat,Md Mahedi Hasan,Md Shamimul Islam,Md Nur Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BaT-SAM参数高效自适应框架，用Conv-LoRA与自提示框回归器实现全自动脑肿瘤分割，仅微调不到5M参数，BRISC2025上加权平均IoU达0.832，并具跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI脑肿瘤分割对诊断与治疗规划至关重要，但人工勾画耗时耗力；现有方法要么任务专用、泛化性有限，要么推理时依赖用户提示。因此需要一种既自动又高泛化的分割方案。

Method: BaT-SAM在冻结的SAM ViT编码器中注入Conv-LoRA，通过稀疏门控的多尺度卷积专家混合处理图像特征，以补偿ViT平移等变归纳偏置的不足，仅微调少于5M参数（不足全模型1%）；并引入轻量自提示框回归器，用调度采样与适配器联合优化，直接从图像嵌入预测肿瘤位置，去除用户提示依赖。

Result: 在BRISC2025基准上加权平均IoU为0.832，超越现有最优方法；跨数据集泛化表现强，Figshare上Dice为0.702，LGG MRI Segmentation数据集上Dice为0.784。

Conclusion: BaT-SAM以参数高效方式实现全自动脑肿瘤分割，兼顾高精度与跨数据集泛化，验证了将局部归纳偏置与自提示机制引入SAM的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate brain tumor segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for diagnosis and treatment planning; however, manual delineation is labor-intensive and time-consuming. Existing approaches are either task-specific with limited generalization or rely on user-provided prompts during inference. We present BaT-SAM, a parameter-efficient adaptation framework that enables fully automatic brain tumor segmentation with the Segment Anything Model (SAM). BaT-SAM injects local inductive bias into SAM's frozen Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder via Conv-LoRA, where image features are processed by a sparsely gated mixture of multi-scale convolutional experts. This design compensates for the limited translation-equivariant inductive bias of vanilla ViTs while fine-tuning fewer than 5M parameters (less than 1% of the full model). To remove the need for user prompts, we further introduce a lightweight self-prompting box regressor that is jointly optimized with the adapter using scheduled sampling and predicts tumor locations directly from image embeddings. On the BRISC2025 benchmark, BaT-SAM achieves a weighted mean IoU of 0.832 while outperforming state-of-the-art benchmarks. It further demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization, achieving Dice scores of 0.702 on Figshare and 0.784 on the LGG MRI Segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [98] [StimFlow: Synthesizing Brain Activity from Images via Stimulus-conditioned Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15341)
*Bui Van Hung,Dang Thanh Vu,Hoang-Son Vo,Mai Duc Minh Huy,Mai Tien Dung,Soo-Hyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StimFlow，一种在原生体素空间用线性路径条件流匹配直接合成fMRI响应的生成式编码器，结合DINOv2结构特征与CLIP语义特征以匹配视觉层级，单样本即达到基线五样本平均的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI响应在试次间存在固有变异性，支持将刺激到响应的映射视为生成问题。但既有生成式编码器多工作在压缩潜空间，且主要依赖语义特征，对早期视觉皮层缺乏低层线索。

Method: StimFlow直接在原生体素空间进行合成，采用带线性路径的条件流匹配（conditional flow matching），使训练与推理分布对齐，从而在单次采样下即可获得有竞争力结果并减少对平均的依赖；同时融合DINOv2结构特征与CLIP语义特征以匹配视觉加工层级。

Result: 单样本下StimFlow取得每位体素Pearson相关0.371，与需平均五次采样的基线相当；在体素空间生成支持无需解码器的直接ROI分析与概念定位；仅用一小时扫描数据即可恢复超过全数据性能的一半，支持高效跨被试适配。

Conclusion: 在原生体素空间进行流匹配生成，并联合结构与语义特征，可有效建模fMRI响应的试次变异性，兼顾单样本性能、可解释性与跨被试适配效率。

Abstract: The inherent variability of fMRI responses across trials encourages a generative view of the stimulus-to-response mapping. Prior generative encoders often operate in compressed latent spaces and condition predominantly on semantic features, which provide limited low-level cues for early visual cortex. We propose StimFlow, which synthesizes fMRI directly in native voxel space using conditional flow matching with a linear path. This design aligns training and inference distributions, enabling competitive performance with a single sample and reducing reliance on averaging. To match the visual hierarchy, StimFlow combines structural DINOv2 features with semantic CLIP features, which prove complementary along the cortical processing stream. With a single sample, StimFlow achieves a per-voxel Pearson correlation of 0.371, matching baselines that average five draws. Generation in voxel space allows direct ROI analysis and concept localization without a decoder. Furthermore, it recovers over half of full-data performance from just one hour of scans, supporting efficient cross-subject adaptation.

</details>


### [99] [Across Viewpoints and Time: Trajectory-Generalizable Physical Attacks on World Models and Video Understanding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15343)
*Song Xia,Meiwen Ding,Chenqi Kong,Wenhan Yang,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TARA 攻击方法，研究单个物理对抗纹理在真实动态视觉环境下对不同视角/轨迹的世界模型与视频理解模型的欺骗性，并构建可复现基准测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型与视频理解模型日益部署于动态视觉环境中，但其对物理对抗威胁的鲁棒性仍鲜有研究；核心问题是单个物理对抗纹理能否在视角随物体-相机轨迹变化的多个真实场景中持续欺骗受害模型。

Method: 提出轨迹感知对抗对齐与精炼攻击（TARA）：包含轨迹感知优化以刻画贴图在真实场景中的几何变化；轨迹感知对比对齐使不同视频-轨迹实例的受攻击表征收敛到共享对抗区域；轨迹感知任务精炼将对齐表征转化为任务级预测误差。同时构建几何约束的跟踪流水线以生成物理合理的对抗区域与时间一致轨迹。

Result: 在 EPIC-KITCHENS-100 与 Something-Something V2 上提供物理合理且时间一致的对抗区域；并采集真实世界的打印-重拍视频验证物理部署条件下的攻击有效性。结果显示单个可打印对抗纹理能在物理条件下持续干扰世界模型与视频理解模型。

Conclusion: 异构视频语义与轨迹导致梯度对齐不佳，TARA 通过轨迹感知的对齐与精炼有效解决该问题；所构建的标注基准与真实采集数据组成可复现测试平台，揭示物理对抗纹理对世界模型与视频理解模型构成根本性安全挑战。

Abstract: World models and video understanding models are increasingly deployed in dynamic visual environments, yet their robustness to physical adversarial threats remains largely underexplored. We study whether a single physical adversarial texture can mislead the victim model across different real-world scenarios where observing viewpoints vary along uncontrolled object--camera trajectories. We identify that heterogeneous video semantics and trajectories induce poorly aligned gradients for optimizing the adversarial texture. To address that, we propose the Trajectory-aware adversarial Alignment and Refinement Attack (TARA), which incorporates trajectory-aware optimization to capture the evolving geometric variations of a printed texture under real-world scenarios. To improve cross-instance optimization compatibility, TARA incorporates trajectory-aware contrastive alignment to encourage attacked representations from diverse video--trajectory instances to approach a shared adversarial region and then performs trajectory-aware task refinement to translate the aligned representation into task-level prediction errors. For reproducible evaluation, we develop a geometry-grounded tracking pipeline that provides physically plausible adversarial regions with temporally consistent trajectories on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 and Something-Something V2. We further collect real-world print-and-recapture videos to validate attack effectiveness under physical deployment conditions. Together, the annotated benchmarks and real-world captured data form a reproducible testbed for future evaluation. Notably, our results reveal that a single printable adversarial texture can persistently disrupt world models and video understanding models under physical conditions, highlighting the fundamental security challenge.

</details>


### [100] [Video-Based Sexual Harassment Detection Using Fine-Tuned Video Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15354)
*Jubayer- Al- Mahmud,Md Shamimul Islam,Md Nur Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 扩展SHV数据集至600段视频，微调三种视频Transformer进行性骚扰二分类检测，TimeSformer取得最高92.27%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性骚扰是普遍的社会问题，大量事件因证据不足和干预延迟而未被举报。实时检测的智能监控系统可辅助执法并起威慑作用。但视频性骚扰检测面临服装、光照、遮挡、低分辨率、视角差异等挑战，且因缺乏大规模标注视频数据集而研究有限。

Method: 将Sexual Harassment Video (SHV)数据集扩展至600段来自YouTube的短视频，按统一标注准则划分为骚扰与非骚扰两类；对在Kinetics-400上预训练的三种SOTA视频Transformer模型（VideoMAE、ViViT、TimeSformer）进行微调，用于二分类检测。

Result: 三个模型均取得有竞争力的分类性能：TimeSformer测试准确率最高，达92.27%，优于VideoMAE的91.71%和ViViT的91.16%（基于扩展SHV测试集）。性能受数据集规模、视频质量、帧采样策略和模型架构显著影响。

Conclusion: 本工作对现代视频Transformer在此敏感真实任务上进行了全面评估，扩展的数据集与基准结果为该方向提供了参考。

Abstract: Sexual harassment represents a pervasive societal challenge, with a substantial proportion of incidents remaining unreported due to insufficient evidence and delayed intervention mechanisms. Smart surveillance systems capable of detecting such incidents in real-time could assist law enforcement and serve as a deterrent. However, video-based harassment detection poses significant challenges, including variations in clothing, illumina- tion, partial occlusions, low resolution, and diverse camera view- points. Despite its social importance, this problem has received limited research attention, primarily due to the scarcity of large- scale annotated video datasets. To address this gap, we extend the Sexual Harassment Video (SHV) dataset to 600 short video clips sourced from YouTube and annotated into harassment and non-harassment categories using consistent labeling guidelines. We fine-tune three state-of-the-art video transformer models, VideoMAE, ViViT, and TimeSformer, pretrained on Kinetics- 400, for binary harassment classification. Experimental results show that all three models achieve competitive classification performance, with TimeSformer attaining the highest test accu- racy of 92.27%, outperforming VideoMAE 91.71% and ViViT 91.16% on the extended SHV test set. Our analysis reveals that performance is notably influenced by dataset size, video quality, frame sampling strategy, and model architecture. This work presents a comprehensive evaluation of modern video transformers for this sensitive real-world task.

</details>


### [101] [UniForge3D: Joint 3D Reconstruction and Generation in a Shared Anchor-Guided Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15357)
*Wenjue Chen,Wenyue Chen,Heliang Zheng,Rongfei Jia,Yuan Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniForge3D 提出在共享锚点引导空间中联合建模重建与生成，解决坐标不匹配与多视图特征混杂问题，实现从稀疏无位姿图像生成完整高质量3D资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、无位姿图像生成完整3D资产需同时保留观测几何并补全未观测区域。联合重建与生成可兼顾两者：重建恢复输入图像中的几何，生成提供学习到的形状先验。但相机相对的重建与规范空间的生成存在坐标不匹配，且对所有视图不加区分地条件化会混合有用几何证据与无关图像内容，影响局部细节恢复。

Method: 提出 UniForge3D，在共享的锚点引导空间中联合建模重建与生成以消除坐标不匹配，该空间采用锚点视图方位角并保持物体直立。两个分支双向交换特征，预测逐视图点图与完整稀疏体素结构；进一步以生成结构条件化点图预测，建立像素到体素对应关系，并为每个体素选择信息量最大的输入视图，用所选视图特征引导细节形状生成。

Result: 在 Toys4K 和 GSO 数据集上取得最先进的外观与几何结果，生成的完整3D资产具有更高的保真度。

Conclusion: 通过锚点引导共享空间与双向特征交换、视图选择机制，UniForge3D 有效统一了重建与生成，提升了稀疏无位姿图像下完整3D资产生成的保真度。

Abstract: Generating complete 3D assets from sparse, unposed images requires preserving observed geometry while completing unseen regions. Joint reconstruction and generation offers a way to address both goals: reconstruction recovers the geometry observed in the input images, while generation contributes learned shape priors. However, combining camera-relative reconstruction with canonical-space generation introduces a coordinate mismatch that makes direct geometric interaction more difficult. Moreover, conditioning each 3D location on all views without accounting for their relevance may mix useful geometric evidence with unrelated image content, hindering local detail recovery. We present UniForge3D, which jointly models reconstruction and generation in a shared anchor-guided space, resolving their coordinate mismatch.
