Method: 提出 MemAct 框架：单一视觉-语言模型增量写入结构化历史记忆，按需检索历史记录与过去帧，并通过移动、转向获取额外证据；检索到的证据与动作观测共同指导后续决策，在统一推理循环中完成继续探索或作答。构建 MemAct 套件（真实与仿真环境的数据生成管线与数据集合），约 1M 训练样本覆盖空间推理、记忆写入、检索与探索；MemAct-bench 含 20,000 主问答对（评测当前观测理解、历史证据使用、主动探索）及 2,000 个独立 Timing 问题。训练上结合监督微调（记忆与交互）与反事实动作优化，以累积证据增益、动作成本与最终答案质量为目标。

Result: 实验表明 MemAct 优于现有空间理解与流式基线方法，在 MemAct-bench 上接近前沿多模态模型，同时保持流式推理效率。

Conclusion: 将记忆写入/检索与主动探索动作纳入统一流式推理循环，可有效缓解流式空间理解中主动探索不足的问题，并在性能与流式效率间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is essential for embodied agents navigating and interacting with the physical world. As observations arrive incrementally, agents must reason in a streaming manner. Yet streaming understanding must also be closed-loop: task-relevant evidence may require both recalling past observations and actively acquiring new views. Existing streaming approaches largely emphasize processing incoming observations, leaving task-driven active exploration underexplored. We introduce MemAct, a framework for streaming spatial understanding with memory and action. A shared vision--language model incrementally writes structured historical memory, retrieves records and past frames on demand, and acquires additional evidence through movement and turning. Retrieved evidence and action observations inform subsequent decisions, enabling the agent to continue exploring or answer within a unified reasoning loop. We develop the MemAct suite, a data generation pipeline and collection across real and simulated environments, with approximately 1M training examples for spatial reasoning, memory writing, retrieval, and exploration. Its MemAct-bench comprises 20,000 main QA pairs evaluating current-observation understanding, historical evidence use, and active exploration, plus 2,000 separate Timing questions. MemAct combines supervised fine-tuning for memory and interaction with counterfactual action optimization using cumulative evidence gains, action costs, and terminal answer quality to improve exploration and stopping decisions. Experiments show that MemAct outperforms existing spatial understanding and streaming baselines and approaches frontier multimodal models on MemAct-bench while retaining streaming inference efficiency.

</details>


### [269] [Open-o3-Video-v2: Seek, Ground, and Reason with Verifiable Spatio-Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17656)
*Jiahao Meng,Zhongyuan Bao,Yue Tan,Haiquan Wen,Renrui Zhang,Anna Wang,Jason Li,Yanwei Fu,Yunhai Tong,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open-o3-Video-v2，将视频推理建模为统一的智能体式“证据落地”过程：模型主动识别缺失信息、获取额外观测、定位支撑推理的时刻与物体并把证据写入推理轨迹。配套构建 STGR-v2-CoT135K（含 52K 多轮智能体落地推理轨迹）与 STGR-v2-RL-13K，并提出 Clue-Seek GRPO (CS-GRPO) 强化学习策略。在 V-STaR 上，4B 模型取得 40.4% mAM 与 56.3% mLGM，较 Qwen3-VL-4B 主干分别提升 8.5% 与 12.6%，并在通用视频理解、长视频推理与时间定位任务上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的视频推理不仅要求答案正确，还需给出支撑性的视觉证据。已有工作 Open-o3-Video 虽通过将时间戳与物体级边界框嵌入推理轨迹实现可验证推理，但其观测受限于初始稀疏采样器所抽取的帧，无法在推理中途主动补充缺失信息。

Method: 将视频推理形式化为统一的智能体式证据落地（agentic evidence-grounded）流程：模型识别缺失信息、主动获取额外观测、对支撑推理的时刻与物体进行定位，并将证据写入轨迹后再继续推理或作答。为支持该行为，构建 STGR-v2-CoT135K 数据集（含 52K 条多轮智能体落地推理轨迹）与 STGR-v2-RL-13K 强化学习数据。进一步提出 Clue-Seek GRPO（CS-GRPO），追踪工具调用如何改变累积证据，奖励线索覆盖与逐步增益，并惩罚过度、重复或无信息量的检索。

Result: 在 V-STaR 基准上，Open-o3-Video-v2-4B 达到 40.4% mAM 与 56.3% mLGM，分别超过其 Qwen3-VL-4B 主干 8.5% 与 12.6%；同时在通用视频理解、长视频推理与时间定位等基准上获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，主动式证据获取与可验证的物体级落地在可靠视频推理中具有互补性，二者结合可显著提升模型性能。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Reliable video reasoning requires models to produce correct answers and expose supporting visual evidence. Open-o3-Video enables verifiable reasoning by embedding timestamps and object-level bounding boxes into reasoning traces, but is restricted to frames from an initial sparse sampler. We introduce Open-o3-Video-v2, which formulates video reasoning as a unified agentic evidencegrounded process. The model identifies missing information, acquires additional observations, grounds the moments and objects supporting its reasoning, and writes this evidence into the trajectory before continuing or answering. To support this behavior, we construct STGR-v2-CoT135K, including 52K multi-turn agentic grounded reasoning trajectories, and STGR-v2-RL-13K for reinforcement learning. We further propose Clue-Seek GRPO (CS-GRPO), which tracks how tool calls change accumulated evidence, rewards clue coverage and step-wise gains, and penalizes excessive, repetitive, or uninformative retrieval. On V-STaR, Open-o3-Video-v2-4B achieves 40.4% mAM and 56.3% mLGM, outperforming its Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 8.5% and 12.6%, respectively. It also delivers consistent gains across general video understanding, long-video reasoning, and temporal grounding benchmarks. These results show that active evidence acquisition and verifiable object-level grounding are complementary for reliable video reasoning. The code and models are released at https://msalab-pku.github.io/projects/Open-o3-Video-v2.

</details>


### [270] [ScenePercept: Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Assessment of Real-World User-Generated Content Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17661)
*Shuhan Xia,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Peipie Li,Zekun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenePercept-Bench基准（23,100张图像、21大类、105个细粒度场景、15个属性、含总分与结构化推理标注），并设计场景条件感知对齐（SCPA）训练框架，联合优化绝对分数预测、偏好排序与跨场景校准，在UGC图像感知上达到SOTA并有效泛化到IQA/IAA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动成像与社交平台发展，UGC图像爆发式增长，亟需系统全面的感知评估。然而现有图像感知基准多为任务导向，针对特定场景（质量或美学）构建，并对所有场景施加统一的感知标准，难以刻画真实UGC图像的场景多样性与感知复杂性。

Method: 1）构建ScenePercept-Bench：覆盖真实UGC分布，含23,100张图像、21个类别、105个细粒度场景；2）设计分层、场景感知的标注协议，涵盖视觉质量、美学感知与场景语义共15个属性，每张图像标注总体分数、15个属性分数及结构化解释性推理；3）提出场景条件感知对齐（SCPA）训练框架，联合优化绝对分数预测、偏好排序与跨场景校准，以应对场景间感知标准异质性。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCPA在UGC图像感知任务上取得state-of-the-art性能，并能有效泛化到现有IQA与IAA基准。

Conclusion: 通过场景多样化的基准与场景条件对齐的训练框架，本文缓解了统一感知标准带来的局限，在保持模型通用感知评估能力的同时促使其探索并适应不同场景的感知标准，为UGC图像综合感知评估提供了新的基准与方法。

Abstract: The rapid development of mobile imaging and social platforms has led to explosive growth in user-generated content images, making their systematic and comprehensive perceptual assessment increasingly important. Existing image perception benchmarks are typically task-oriented, curating specific scenarios for quality or aesthetic assessment and applying a uniform perceptual criterion across diverse scenarios. This limits their ability to capture the scene diversity and perceptual complexity of real-world UGC images. To address these limitations, we introduce ScenePercept-Bench, a benchmark designed to reflect the distribution of real-world UGC images, comprising 23,100 images across 21 categories and 105 fine-grained scenes. To account for the influence of scene context on human perception, we design a hierarchical, scene-aware annotation protocol, covering visual quality, aesthetic perception, and scene semantics, with 15 attributes in total. Each image is annotated with an overall score, 15 attribute-level scores, and structured explanatory reasoning. Building on this benchmark, we further introduce Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Alignment, a training framework that tackles the heterogeneity of perceptual criteria across scenarios. SCPA jointly optimizes absolute score prediction, preference ranking, and cross-scenario calibration, preserving the model general perceptual assessment ability while encouraging it to explore and adapt perceptual criteria across different scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SCPA achieves state-of-the-art performance in UGC image perception and generalizes effectively to established IQA and IAA benchmarks.

</details>


### [271] [Hierarchical Curriculum Learning for Surgical Video Generation: Focusing on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach Robotic Thyroidectomy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17684)
*Youngbin Kong,Seungho Lee,Jae Kyung Shin,Jong-hyuk Ahn,Hyoun-Joong Kong,Kyu Eun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合分层手术标注与课程迁移学习的手术视频生成框架，在BABA机器人甲状腺切除术上将FVD降低20.1%、FID降低15.7%，并验证收益源于课程设计而非训练量增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频生成对手术教育、仿真与下游AI训练日益重要，但传统仿真流程受成本、真实感与场景多样性限制。将通用文本到视频扩散模型迁移至手术场景存在困难：解剖结构复杂、器械-组织交互精细、手术流程本身具有层级性，且除少数研究充分的术式外缺乏整理好的训练数据。

Method: 提出融合分层术式标注与课程迁移学习的手术视频生成框架。将手术流程分解为三个阶段层级（phase、task、action），让预训练文本到视频扩散模型由粗到细逐步适配手术语境；并引入动态采样策略以处理变分辨率、变长度的手术视频片段。在BABA（双侧腋乳入路）机器人甲状腺切除术上实例化该框架，该术式此前无整理好的基准。

Result: 相较此前的task级模型，最终的action级模型FVD降低20.1%、FID降低15.7%；在相同预算的消融实验中，这些提升源于课程设计而非额外训练。六名临床医生的用户研究对视觉真实感评价较高，同时指出在解剖准确性与临床可靠性方面仍存差距。

Conclusion: 该框架为在整理数据稀缺的术式中实现AI驱动的手术仿真迈出了一步。

Abstract: High-fidelity surgical video generation is increasingly important for surgical education, simulation, and downstream AI training, as conventional simulation pipelines are limited by cost, realism, and scenario diversity. Adapting generic text-to-video diffusion models to surgical procedures remains difficult, however, due to complex anatomical structures, intricate tool-tissue interactions, the inherently hierarchical nature of surgical workflows, and the scarcity of curated training data outside a few well-studied procedures. We propose a surgical video generation framework that combines hierarchical procedural annotation with curriculum transfer learning. Surgical procedures are decomposed into three levels (phase, task, and action) and a pretrained text-to-video diffusion model is progressively adapted from coarse to fine-grained surgical context, supported by a dynamic sampling strategy that handles variable-resolution and variable-length surgical clips. We instantiate the framework on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach robotic thyroidectomy, a procedure for which no curated benchmark currently exists. Relative to the preceding task-level model, the final action-level model reduces FVD by 20.1% and FID by 15.7%, and a matched-budget ablation indicates that these gains arise from the curriculum design rather than from additional training. A user study with six clinicians rates visual realism highly while highlighting remaining gaps in anatomical accuracy and clinical reliability. The proposed framework offers a step toward AI-driven surgical simulation in procedures where curated data are scarce.

</details>


### [272] [SWORD: Scene-Grounded Wasserstein Object-Relation Distance for 3D Scene Graph Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17692)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWORD，一种基于场景图标注构建的Wasserstein对象-关系距离，用于3D场景图预测的语义图距离监督，替代仅依赖精确标签分类的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图预测主要用对象节点分类损失和关系边分类损失优化，只鼓励精确标签恢复，不直接优化与真值场景图的语义接近度；两个错误图即使语义接近度差异很大，也可能获得相近惩罚。直接图距离损失因需图匹配与编辑代价设计而困难。

Method: 利用既有监督协议在固定对象节点索引和监督边槽上的特点，将场景图比较转化为对齐图距离问题。SWORD从训练场景图标注中，通过确定性的“基到上下文”顺序构建块归一化的对象与关系ground distance度量，不依赖外部语义先验；再将其提升为对齐节点边际和零增广边边际上的Wasserstein距离。

Result: SWORD在保持真值图为对齐边际概率空间中唯一零距离解的同时，提供超越精确标签惩罚的语义图距离监督；并证明其在对齐边际概率场景图上为严格度量，保持场景图grounded替换排序，控制加性graph-grounded语义可观测量，并产生解码语义误差的代理上界。

Conclusion: SWORD将3D场景图预测中的图比较建模为对齐边际概率空间上的Wasserstein距离，实现语义感知的图距离监督，并具备度量性质与误差上界等理论保证。

Abstract: 3D scene graph prediction is commonly optimized with object-node and relation-edge classification losses. These objectives encourage exact object and relation label recovery, but they do not directly optimize semantic proximity to the ground-truth scene graph: two wrong graphs can receive comparable penalties even when one is much closer in object roles and relation contexts. A direct graph-distance loss is difficult in general, since graph matching and graph edit distance require correspondence search and edit-cost design. Our key observation is that prior supervised 3D scene graph prediction protocols are conducted on fixed object-node indices and supervised edge slots. This can turn a scene graph comparison into a well-defined aligned graph-distance problem. To this end, we propose SWORD, a Scene-Grounded Wasserstein Object-Relation Distance that builds block-normalized object and relation ground distance metrics from training scene graph annotations through a deterministic base-to-context order, without external semantic priors. SWORD lifts these metrics to Wasserstein distances over aligned node and null-augmented edge marginals, providing semantic graph-distance supervision beyond exact-label penalties while preserving the ground-truth graph as the unique zero-distance solution in aligned marginal probability space. We prove that SWORD is a strict metric on aligned marginal probabilistic scene graphs, preserves scene-graph-grounded substitution ordering, controls additive graph-grounded semantic observables, and yields a surrogate upper-bounding decoded semantic error.

</details>


### [273] [A Differentiable 3D Scene Graph Metric via Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17705)
*U-Chae Jun,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出上下文Hellinger三元组几何（Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry），一种图级监督目标，用于度量3D场景图预测结果与真值之间的语义与结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图预测通常采用局部物体与谓词分类损失进行监督。这种逐槽位标注预测方式虽有效，但无法提供预测场景图与真值之间的图级语义距离：语义轻微的标签错误与结构破坏性的关系错误会被同等对待，主谓宾事实也未作为场景级单元进行比较。

Method: 将3D场景图预测器的常规分类器输出重新解释为对齐的概率场景图场，其中物体与谓词预测共同定义上下文相关的主谓宾事实。该表述导出可微的基于度量的训练目标，并具有高效因子化实现；可直接应用于现有3D场景图预测架构，无需修改网络设计。

Result: 在3DSSG数据集上的实验表明，该监督方法改善了下游图条件3D场景生成；消融研究证实了所提出图级项的互补作用；用户研究显示其在图描述性、关系合理性和结构保持方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过图级语义与结构差异监督，可弥补逐槽位分类损失的不足，提升3D场景图预测的质量及其在下游任务中的表现。

Abstract: 3D scene graph prediction is commonly supervised with local object and predicate classification losses. Although effective for slot-wise label prediction, this formulation does not provide a graph-level semantic distance between a predicted scene graph and its ground truth: semantically mild label errors and structurally disruptive relational errors can be treated similarly, and subject-predicate-object facts are not compared as scene-level units. We propose Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry, a graph-level supervision objective that measures the semantic and structural discrepancy between 3D scene graphs. Our key idea is to reinterpret the conventional classifier outputs of a 3D scene graph predictor as an aligned probabilistic scene graph field, where object and predicate predictions jointly define contextual subject-predicate-object facts. This formulation yields a differentiable metric-based training objective with an efficient factorized implementation and can be applied to existing 3D scene graph prediction architectures without modifying their network design. Experiments on 3DSSG demonstrate that our supervision improves downstream graph-conditioned 3D scene generation, while ablation studies confirm the complementary roles of the proposed graph-level terms and user studies show better graph descriptiveness, relation plausibility, and structural preservation.

</details>


### [274] [From Token Correction to Claim Admission: Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17706)
*Hyeonggeun Han,Taehyun Cho,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的EGVCA框架，通过比较图像与视觉空输入下的语义声明似然，仅接纳有图像支撑的声明，从而缓解LVLM物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型常出现物体幻觉，即生成输入图像不支持的声明，需要一种有效的缓解手段。

Method: EGVCA：免训练框架，仅当语义声明获得充分的、依赖图像的支持时才接纳。通过比较声明在观测图像与视觉空输入下的似然来度量支持度。用于captioning时，在不改变token生成的情况下控制语义扩展；用于二元VQA时，将原子视觉支持与图像引起的原生Yes-No margin变化相结合。

Result: 在CHAIR、POPE、MME三个基准、三个LVLM骨干上的实验显示：EGVCA显著降低物体幻觉并保持caption质量；在九个骨干-采样设置中有八个取得最高POPE F1；在三个骨干上均提升原生MME总分。

Conclusion: 声明级接纳（claim-level admission）是比token级分布校正更简单的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models often exhibit object hallucination, generating claims that are not supported by the input image. To mitigate this issue, we propose Evidence-Guided Visual Claim Admission (EGVCA), a training-free framework that admits a semantic claim only when it receives sufficient image-dependent support. EGVCA measures this support by comparing the claim likelihood under the observed image and a visual-null input. For captioning, it controls semantic expansion without altering token generation. For binary visual question answering, it combines atomic visual support with the image-induced change in the model's native Yes--No margin. Experiments on CHAIR, POPE, and MME across three LVLM backbones show that EGVCA substantially reduces object hallucination while preserving caption quality. It achieves the highest POPE F1 in eight of nine backbone--sampling settings and improves the native MME total score on all three backbones. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of claim-level admission as a simple alternative to token-level distribution correction.

</details>


### [275] [DT-LoRA: Reaching Beyond the Tangent Space of Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17730)
*Yangming Zhang,Ling Yi,Datong Li,Jinliang Ding,Xiaojie Wang,Tiecheng Song,Zhaolong Ning*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从切空间视角分析LoRA性能不足的原因，提出动态切空间LoRA（DT-LoRA），在不增加参数与推理成本的前提下取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA常弱于全量微调。作者认为原因在于分解式更新被限制在当前低秩因子的切空间内，切空间之外的梯度分量未被利用，从而造成性能差距。

Method: 分析十个代表性LoRA变体，发现残余梯度分量占总梯度能量的大部分且具有强谱集中性。据此提出DT-LoRA：将固定秩预算分为稳定分支与动态分支以捕获主要残余梯度方向；证明这些方向是在正交补与秩约束下局部二次模型的最优更新；采用无矩阵随机SVD恢复方向，避免显式构造残余梯度，并通过受控回写保留累积更新、稳定训练。该方法不增加可训练参数与推理开销。

Result: 在计算机视觉与自然语言处理任务上，DT-LoRA相较LoRA及其变体取得一致改进；在开放词汇域泛化语义分割任务上超越任务特定方法，达到最优结果。

Conclusion: 切空间外的残余梯度是LoRA性能瓶颈的关键来源，DT-LoRA通过动态捕获主残余方向有效缩小与全量微调的差距，且具备通用性与零额外开销的优势。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning. We investigate this performance gap through a tangent-space lens: factorized updates are confined to the tangent space of the current low-rank factors, leaving gradient components outside this space unexploited. An analysis of ten representative LoRA variants reveals that these residual components account for most of the gradient energy and exhibit strong spectral concentration. Motivated by this finding, we propose Dynamic Tangent LoRA (DT-LoRA), which partitions a fixed rank budget into a stable branch and a dynamic branch to capture the principal residual gradient directions. We show that these directions define the optimal update of a local quadratic model under orthogonal-complement and rank constraints. Matrix-free randomized SVD recovers them without materializing the residual gradient, while controlled write-back preserves accumulated updates and stabilizes training within the allocated rank. DT-LoRA adds no trainable parameters or inference cost over LoRA. Experiments across computer vision and natural language processing tasks demonstrate consistent improvements over LoRA and its variants. Moreover, despite being a general-purpose adaptation method, DT-LoRA outperforms task-specific approaches to open-vocabulary domain generalization for semantic segmentation, achieving state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://github.com/Snipine/DT-LoRA.

</details>


### [276] [AttenIR: Aligning Dense Visual Attention with Mesh Warping for Unsupervised Image Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17743)
*Yun Zhang,Zhiruo Zhang,Lifei Zhu,Zhe Zhu,Tong Li,Fang-Lue Zhang,Shengjuan Liu,Xinyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督图像重定向框架 AttenIR，基于内容感知网格变形，耦合稠密视觉注意力与稀疏网格约束，无需人工标注即可保持内容保真与全局结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像重定向方法在保持全局结构与内容保真度方面仍存在不足，且学习型方法虽减少对手工特征与优化流程的依赖，但性能仍有局限。

Method: 将网格变形建模为顶点位移回归任务；以显著性先验为指导，联合优化尺度、结构与几何保持损失；采用由粗到细的投影策略，将稠密视觉注意力先验与稀疏网格约束对齐，实现像素级内容感知变形。

Result: 实验表明，该方法与传统方法及当前最先进的学习型方法相比，取得了具有竞争力且更优的性能。

Conclusion: AttenIR 是一种轻量级无监督图像重定向方法，在无需人工标注的情况下保证内容保真并保持全局结构，其由粗到细投影策略有效实现了稠密注意力与稀疏网格的耦合。

Abstract: Image retargeting aims to resize images while preserving salient content free from distortion and stretching. Recent learning-based solutions reduce reliance on traditional hand-crafted features and optimization procedures, leading to more efficient and generalizable performance. Nevertheless, existing approaches still struggle to preserve global structure and content fidelity. To address these limitations, we propose AttenIR, an unsupervised image retargeting framework requiring no manual labels, based on content-aware mesh warping, which ef- fectively couples dense visual attention with sparse mesh constraints. We for- mulate the mesh deformation as a vertex displacement regression task. Guided by saliency priors, our method jointly optimizes scale, structure, and geometry preservation losses. By combining dense visual attention with semantic features, it achieves robust, unsupervised retargeting without manual annotations. Our primary contributions include (1) a lightweight unsupervised method for image retargeting that ensures content fidelity and preserves global structure, and (2) a coarse-to-fine projection strategy to align dense visual attention priors with sparse mesh constraints, enabling content-aware deformation at the pixel level. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive and superior performance compared with both traditional and state-of-the-art learning-based methods.

</details>


### [277] [When, Where, and Why: Hierarchical Causal Explainability for Video Face Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17791)
*Anubhav Kataria,Shreya Ghosh,Jingwen Ye,Abhinav Dhall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoFace-X-LLM可解释性面部视频理解框架，含ExplainFace弱监督视频-文本语料与H-TCC因果解释方法，在多个任务上取得显著性能提升并提供解释性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频面部行为理解需回答"何时"出现关键表情、"何处"模型关注面部区域、"为何"该证据具有因果必要性三个问题；现有多模态大模型虽能标注和描述视频，但很少提供忠实解释，尤其对随时间变化的小幅面部运动。

Method: 框架含两部分：(1) ExplainFace，从CelebV-HQ视频自动构建的弱监督视频-文本语料，经人工评估与双模型审计（最多2.3%矛盾）验证；(2) H-TCC（分层时序空间因果概念），定位关键时序时刻（When）、面部区域（Where），并通过定向遮挡验证因果必要性（Why）。

Result: 在ExplainFace微调使外观Macro F1从19.48%升至32.87%（+13.39），动作+4.38、时序情绪+6.77、深度伪造检测+11.84 F1；并测量解释的指向提升、面部/边缘提升、稳定性、Adebayo sanity check与必要性测试。

Conclusion: 该框架在两种骨干架构上均获一致增益，超越单纯示例展示，提供多维度解释性度量，模型、代码与语料可申请用于研究。

Abstract: Understanding facial behaviour in video requires answering three fundamental questions: when a key facial expression occurs, where the model attends on the face at that moment, and why that evidence is necessary rather than merely correlated. Today's multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can label and describe video, but they rarely provide faithful explanations, especially for small facial movements that change over time. We present VideoFace-X-LLM, a framework for explainable facial video understanding comprising two key components. First, we introduce ExplainFace, a weakly supervised video-text corpus for facial behaviour understanding, automatically constructed from CelebV-HQ videos and validated through human evaluation and a two-model audit (at most 2.3% contradictions), demonstrating consistent gains across two backbone architectures. Second, H-TCC (Hierarchical Temporal Spatial Causal Concepts), which finds the key temporal moments (When), the facial regions (Where), and verifies its causal necessity (Why) through targeted occlusion. Fine-tuning on ExplainFace raises appearance Macro F1 from 19.48% to 32.87% (+13.39), with gains of +4.38 on actions, +6.77 on temporal emotion, and +11.84 F1 on deepfake detection. We also measure the explanations pointing lift, face/edge lift, stability, an Adebayo sanity check, and a necessity test rather than only showing examples. Our models, code, and corpus are available on request for research use.

</details>


### [278] [MHVF: Training-Free GUI Grounding via Verification-Guided Multi-Hypothesis Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17792)
*Minghua Chen,Xingming Liao,Weiqiang Zhong,Zhuowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的多假设验证框架（MHVF），通过多假设探索与局部视觉验证提升GUI grounding准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding需将自然语言指令映射为界面可操作位置，但密集布局及小而相似的控件易导致模型选中貌似合理却错误的元素；围绕单一预测进行细化可能遗漏其他候选目标。

Method: MHVF结合多假设探索与局部视觉验证，使用单一预训练视觉语言模型。先从多个全屏预测构建空间假设，通过放大再定位检查对应区域；再在带标记的局部视图中验证候选与指令的一致性以消解歧义；验证分数指导第二轮局部搜索，并对两轮搜索结果进行候选排序与最终选择。

Result: 在八个基准设置、五种通用及GUI专用模型上，多数设置中grounding准确率获得提升：UI-Venus-Ground-7B在Mind2Web与VisualWebBench上分别提升4.3和2.1个百分点；Qwen2.5-VL-7B在ScreenSpot-Pro上由23.6%提升至53.6%。

Conclusion: MHVF作为一种免训练框架，能有效缓解单一预测细化的局限，通过多假设探索与局部验证显著提升GUI grounding准确率。

Abstract: GUI grounding enables web agents to translate natural-language instructions into actionable interface locations. However, dense layouts and small, visually similar controls can lead models toward plausible but incorrect elements, while refinement around a single prediction may leave alternative targets unexplored. We propose the Multi-Hypothesis Verification Framework (MHVF) for training-free GUI grounding. It combines multi-hypothesis exploration with local visual verification using a single pretrained vision-language model. To explore alternative targets, MHVF constructs spatial hypotheses from multiple full-screen predictions and examines the corresponding regions through zoomed re-localization. To resolve ambiguity among the resulting candidates, it verifies their agreement with the instruction in marked local views. Verification scores then guide a second round of local search and rank candidates retained from both search levels for final selection. Experiments with five general-purpose and GUI-specific models across eight benchmark settings demonstrate improvements in grounding accuracy in most settings. MHVF improves UI-Venus-Ground-7B by 4.3 and 2.1 percentage points on Mind2Web and VisualWebBench, respectively, and raises Qwen2.5-VL-7B's accuracy on ScreenSpot-Pro from 23.6% to 53.6%.

</details>


### [279] [Multimodal Fusion of RGB and Complementary Modalities for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17800)
*Hu Cao,Yifeng Cheng,Mu He,Mengyu Li,Yinlong Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向极性感知跨模态融合模块（BPCF）用于RGB-X多模态语义分割，在六种模态组合、八个公开数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-X多模态语义分割通过引入热成像、LiDAR、深度、事件、偏振或光场等辅助传感流，可提升弱光与运动模糊下的鲁棒性；但不同传感器产生的特征往往难以匹配，融合过程易不稳定。

Method: 提出双向极性感知跨模态融合（BPCF）模块，在捕捉互补线索的同时增强特征对齐。在RGB-热成像、RGB-LiDAR、RGB-深度、RGB-事件、RGB-偏振、RGB-光场六种模态配对上进行评估。

Result: 在MFNet、KITTI-360、DELIVER、DDD17、DSEC、MCubeS、ZJU、UrbanLF八个公开数据集上取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: BPCF能有效缓解跨模态特征不匹配与融合不稳定问题，验证了方法的通用性与有效性；代码与训练模型将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Multi-modal semantic segmentation adds an auxiliary sensing stream to RGB images (RGB-X), such as thermal, LiDAR, depth, event, polarization, or light field. This can improve robustness in poor lighting and under motion blur. But different sensors often produce features that do not match well, and fusion can become unstable. To alleviate these issues, we propose a bidirectional polarity-aware cross-modality fusion (BPCF) module that effectively captures complementary cues while enhancing feature alignment. We evaluate the framework on six modality pairings: RGB-Thermal, RGB-LiDAR, RGB-Depth, RGB-Event, RGB-Polarization, and RGB-Light Field. Our model achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on eight public datasets, including MFNet, KITTI-360, DELIVER, DDD17, DSEC, MCubeS, ZJU, and UrbanLF. The codes and trained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [280] [SPARCL: Spatially Adaptive Regional Coverage for Active Learning in Chest X-ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17808)
*Juho Jung,Seongjae Kang,Mijoo Kim,Sungduk Cho,Yunkyoung Jun,Sunghoon Joo,Yoona Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPARCL主动学习框架，通过空间自适应区域覆盖动态平衡预测不确定性与累积空间覆盖，以更少标注达到全监督性能，并引入SI、PC两个指标量化解剖公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光病灶分割的像素级标注成本高昂，主动学习（AL）因此十分必要；但现有AL策略未考虑所选样本的空间分布，导致在局灶性（如主动脉钙化）与弥漫性（如结节）病理上性能不一致，且Dice等聚合指标掩盖了区域失败问题。

Method: SPARCL（Spatially Adaptive Regional Coverage for Active Learning）动态平衡预测不确定性与累积空间覆盖；采用凹覆盖函数对已标注区域施加递减收益，将标注预算导向标注不足区域；通过熵门在空间变化缺失时回退到标准不确定性采样；覆盖目标为单调子模函数，保证贪心批量选择的近最优性。另提出空间不一致性（SI）与性能覆盖率（PC）两个指标衡量多轮AL中的解剖公平性。

Result: 在自建钙化队列与公开CXR基准上评估：SPARCL仅用24%数据即达到全监督性能的95%，最优基线需29%；SPARCL保证了稳健的空间可靠性，揭示出聚合指标所遗漏的区域性增益。

Conclusion: SPARCL通过空间自适应的区域覆盖策略，在降低标注成本的同时提升解剖公平性与空间可靠性，弥补了传统不确定性采样对空间分布的忽视以及聚合评价指标的盲区。

Abstract: Pixel-level annotation for chest X-ray (CXR) lesion segmentation is expensive, making active learning (AL) essential. However, existing AL strategies fail to account for the spatial distribution of selected samples, resulting in inconsistent performance across focal (e.g., aortic calcification) and diffuse (e.g., nodules) pathologies. In this paper, we propose SPARCL (Spatially Adaptive Regional Coverage for Active Learning) to dynamically balance predictive uncertainty with cumulative spatial coverage. By employing a concave coverage function, SPARCL imposes diminishing returns on previously annotated regions, naturally directing the annotation budget toward under-labeled areas. Furthermore, an entropy gate reverts the algorithm to standard uncertainty sampling when spatial variation is absent. This coverage objective is monotone submodular, guaranteeing near-optimal greedy batch selection. To address how aggregate metrics like Dice obscure regional failures, we further introduce two metrics---Spatial Inconsistency (SI) and Performance Coverage (PC)---to quantify anatomical fairness across AL rounds. Evaluated on an in-house calcification cohort and public CXR benchmarks, SPARCL reaches 95% of fully supervised performance using only 24% of the data, compared to 29% for the best baseline. Ultimately, SPARCL ensures robust spatial reliability, surfacing regional gains that aggregate metrics miss.

</details>


### [281] [QK-Edit: Revisiting Attention-based Injection in MM-DiT for Image and Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17829)
*Tiancheng Shen,Zilong Huang,Xiangtai Li,Zhijie Lin,Jiyang Liu,Yitong Wang,Jiashi Feng,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Jun Hao Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QK-Edit，一种面向MM-DiT架构的免训练注意力操控编辑框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-DiT通过多模态自注意力（MM-SA）将图像与文本token拼接处理，已在生成任务上超越U-Net架构；但该机制使传统基于U-Net的注意力操控方法失效，导致缺乏有效的文本引导编辑手段。

Method: 提出免训练的QK-Edit框架，利用MM-DiT特有的注意力动态，设计新颖的query-key操控策略，隔离并调整关键注意力分量，以在提示保真度与结构一致性之间取得最优平衡；可在推理阶段以特征替换方式便捷实现。

Result: 支持物体添加、移除、替换、背景变更、材质变更、颜色变更及风格转换等多类任务；在FLUX、HunyuanVideo等先进模型上取得更优的编辑性能，覆盖图像与视频编辑。

Conclusion: QK-Edit填补了MM-DiT生成能力与可编辑灵活性之间的空白，为可扩展的多模态内容创作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have recently emerged as a powerful framework for unified text-vision synthesis, surpassing traditional U-Net architectures in generative tasks. One key innovation lies in its Multimodal Self-Attention (MM-SA) interaction, where image and text tokens are concatenated and processed via self-attention. However, this mechanism poses significant challenges for editing, rendering conventional U-Net-based attention manipulation methods ineffective. To address this limitation, we propose QK-Edit, a training-free framework that exploits the unique attention dynamics of MM-DiTs for precise text-guided image and video editing. By introducing a novel query-key manipulation strategy, our method isolates and adjusts critical attention components to achieve an optimal balance between prompt fidelity and structural consistency. This enables seamless edits across various tasks, including object addition, object removal, object replacement, changing background, changing material, changing color, and style transformation. Notably, it can be easily implemented with feature replacement in inference. QK-Edit demonstrates superior editing performance on state-of-the-art models, such as FLUX and HunyuanVideo, effectively bridging the gap between generative power and editable flexibility in MM-DiTs, and paving the way for scalable multimodal content creation.

</details>


### [282] [What Makes a Good Visual Representation for Spatial Understanding in MLLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17844)
*Xiaohu Huang,Yumin Guo,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了"什么样的视觉表示有利于多模态大模型(MLLM)的空间理解"，将迁移内容与迁移方式解耦分析，发现表示的3D结构强度与其增益强相关，且表示形式决定所需迁移机制，最终据此蒸馏潜在特征与显式几何到单一MLLM，在3D/4D推理基准上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM视觉预训练数据几乎全为2D，视觉token缺乏固有3D结构信息，难以推理3D物理世界。现有方法通过从外部视觉或几何模型迁移表示来注入结构，但"什么样的视觉表示才有利于空间理解"这一根本问题尚缺乏系统探索。

Method: 将"迁移什么"与"如何迁移"解耦。迁移内容涵盖3D几何模型、自监督图像/视频模型、视觉语言模型和生成模型的潜在特征或显式深度/点图；迁移方式为蒸馏、作为监督目标或作为额外输入融合。通过对照实验将性能差异追溯至表示本身的属性。

Result: 表示的3D结构与增益强相关：潜在特征以跨视角一致性衡量，显式几何以深度与重建精度衡量。表示形式决定迁移机制：潜在特征从融合与蒸馏中获益相当；显式几何从融合中获益甚微，其蒸馏增益主要依赖几何解码头。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，将潜在特征与显式几何同时蒸馏进单一MLLM，仅以RGB视频为输入，在静态3D与动态4D空间推理基准上均稳定超越基座模型。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is a necessary step in moving multimodal large language models (MLLMs) toward reasoning about the 3D physical world. Their visual pretraining data, however, is almost entirely 2D, so the visual tokens processed by MLLMs often lack inherent 3D structural information. A growing number of methods inject such structure by transferring representations from external vision or geometry models, and collectively establish that such transfer helps. Behind these methods lies a largely underexplored question: what makes a visual representation good for spatial understanding? To answer it, we first separate what is transferred from how it is transferred, and then trace the resulting differences back to properties of the representations themselves. What we transfer comes from 3D geometry models, self-supervised image and video models, vision-language models, and generative models, in the form of latent features or explicit depth and point maps. We transfer it either by distillation, using it as a supervision target, or by fusion, feeding it as an additional input. We find that a representation's 3D structure correlates strongly with how much it helps, measured by cross-view consistency for latent features and by depth and reconstruction accuracy for explicit geometry. We further show that the form of a representation dictates the mechanism it needs. Latent features gain as much from fusion as from distillation; explicit geometry gains little from fusion, and most of its distillation gain depends on a geometry decoding head. Guided by these findings, we distill both latent features and explicit geometry into a single MLLM. Taking only RGB video as input, it consistently improves over its base model on both static 3D and dynamic 4D spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [283] [DVTR-KD: Distilling Vision-Language Models via Intermediate Visual Token Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17853)
*Haolan Wei,Jiahui Wang,Junli Zha,Xinkai Lu,Yunxiao Zou,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DVTR-KD，一种基于稠密视觉令牌重构的知识蒸馏框架，解决教师与学生因视觉令牌数不匹配而难以进行逐令牌知识迁移的问题，在保持视觉令牌压缩推理效率的同时提升压缩学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）性能强但计算开销大，难以在边缘设备等资源受限场景部署。知识蒸馏（KD）可从强教师模型向学生迁移知识以减小模型规模；视觉令牌压缩是另一条提升效率的互补路径，但会造成教师与学生视觉令牌数量不匹配，使逐令牌的知识迁移变得困难。

Method: 提出 DVTR-KD（Dense Visual Token Reconstruction 知识蒸馏框架），从学生的压缩令牌重构教师的稠密视觉令牌。具体地：在最终 LLM 层之后以及被识别为对视觉信息读取重要的中间层输入处重构视觉状态；在中间层，重构由教师的响应预测监督，而非直接使用逐令牌的 KL 或 MSE 匹配；并辅以注意力读取损失，对齐学生与原始教师在预测响应时整合上下文信息的方式。

Result: 在多种基准上的评估表明，DVTR-KD 提升了令牌压缩学生模型的性能，同时保留了视觉令牌压缩带来的推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过稠密视觉令牌重构与响应级监督、注意力读取对齐，DVTR-KD 有效缓解了令牌数不匹配带来的迁移障碍，实现了性能与效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on diverse vision-language tasks, but their computational demands limit deployment under constrained budgets, particularly on edge devices. Many works focus on Knowledge Distillation (KD) and try to transfer knowledge from powerful teacher models to students. In addition to reducing model size, visual token compression provides a complementary route to efficiency, but introduces mismatched visual token counts that complicate direct token-wise knowledge transfer. To mitigate this, we propose DVTR-KD, a knowledge distillation framework based on \textbf{D}ense \textbf{V}isual \textbf{T}oken \textbf{R}econstruction, which reconstructs the teacher's dense visual tokens from the student's compressed tokens. We reconstruct visual states after the final LLM layer and at the input to an intermediate layer identified as important for visual-information readout. At the intermediate layer, reconstruction is supervised through the teacher's response predictions rather than by direct token-wise KL or MSE matching. An auxiliary attention-readout loss complements this response-level supervision by aligning how the student and the original teacher integrate contextual information when predicting responses. Evaluations on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DVTR-KD improves the performance of token-compressed students while retaining the inference efficiency afforded by visual token compression.

</details>


### [284] [Frequency Routing and Identity Conditioning for Thermal-to-Visible Face Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17859)
*Haojie Li,Xinlong Sun,Xiaohaoyang Lei,Kai Cheng,Yiguo Qiao,Xu Yang,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FreqID-Control：一种参数高效的热-可见光人脸翻译框架，按频谱可靠性与语义角色组织热成像引导，兼顾图像保真度与身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热-可见光人脸翻译在夜间生物识别、低光监控与热图人工解读中有重要应用，但热图不直接记录可见光外观，且跨谱线索在不同频率分量与语义角色上存在差异。现有空间条件模型以同质适配路径处理这些线索，未显式分离局部结构与全局身份。

Method: FreqID-Control：保持预训练生成先验，包含两部分——频率路由低秩适配（为低频结构和高频细节分配独立变换子空间）；共享跨谱身份条件（在空间热标记之外提供紧凑的全局身份标记）。设计依据来自 MCXFace 与 SpeakingFaces 上的受控分析，发现低频对应稳定、细节对应集中于边缘区域。

Result: 在两个数据集上实现较强的图像保真度与生物身份保持；MCXFace 上的受控消融验证了频率路由与共享身份条件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 按频率可靠性与语义角色解耦热引导、并分离局部结构与全局身份，是提升热-可见光人脸翻译质量与身份保持的有效且参数高效的方案。

Abstract: Thermal-to-visible (T2V) face translation has important applications in nighttime biometric recognition, low-light surveillance, and human interpretation of thermal observations. The task remains challenging because thermal images do not directly record visible appearance, while the cross-spectral cues useful for reconstruction vary across frequency components and semantic roles. Existing spatially conditioned models tend to process these cues through a homogeneous adaptation path and do not explicitly separate local structure from global identity. We propose FreqID-Control, a parameter-efficient framework that organizes thermal guidance according to spectral reliability and semantic role while preserving the pretrained generative prior. It combines frequency-routed low-rank adaptation, which allocates separate transformation subspaces to low-frequency structure and high-frequency detail, with shared cross-spectral identity conditioning, which supplies compact global identity tokens independently of spatial thermal tokens. A controlled analysis on MCXFace and SpeakingFaces provides the empirical basis for this design, revealing stable low-frequency correspondence and edge-localized detail correspondence. Experiments on both datasets demonstrate strong image fidelity and biometric identity preservation, while controlled ablations on MCXFace confirm the complementary contributions of frequency routing and shared identity conditioning.

</details>


### [285] [MicroCLIP: Connecting Text and Microscopy Images via Contrastive Language--Image Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17866)
*Yanran Ruan,Zhixiang Wei,Shiyao Hong,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yuxuan Gu,Liming Ye,Huaian Chen,Yi Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MicroCLIP：基于自然语言监督的荧光显微图像视觉-语言基础模型，配合2.5M图文对数据集MicroPool-2M，通过类别级、知识级、细节级三层对齐实现开放集分类与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有荧光显微图像理解方法多从闭集数据中学习预定义概念，受限于任务与生物语境，难以跨任务迁移。作者希望借助自然语言监督学习可泛化的细胞与亚细胞表征，支持开放集分类与检索。

Method: 提出MicroCLIP视觉-语言模型，并构建MicroPool-2M数据集（约250万荧光显微图像-文本对，涵盖多种细胞结构、细胞系与成像条件）。针对显微图像缺乏描述性标注与生物知识标注的问题，采用三层渐进式图文关联对齐：(1) 类别级对齐，将图像链接到"亚细胞结构-细胞系"组合定义的标准化标签；(2) 知识级对齐，引入由标签衍生的生物先验以刻画跨物种、跨细胞类型的关系；(3) 细节级对齐，利用多模态大语言模型大规模重写并核验图文对，强调细粒度视觉特征。

Result: 在显微图像零样本分类与文本-图像检索任务上取得领先性能；可从上千候选中检索具有特定细胞特征的图像，并在无额外监督下泛化到未见过的生物概念。

Conclusion: MicroCLIP展示了作为荧光显微图像基础模型的潜力，为自动化生物研究提供可迁移的开放集通用表征。

Abstract: Understanding fluorescence microscopy images is fundamental to automating biological research. However, existing methods typically learn predefined concepts from closed-set data, limiting transfer across tasks and biological contexts. In this work, we introduce MicroCLIP, a vision-language model that learns generalizable cellular and subcellular representations from natural language supervision for open-set image classification and retrieval. To pretrain MicroCLIP, we construct MicroPool-2M, a dataset of approximately 2.5 million fluorescence microscopy image-text pairs spanning diverse cellular structures, cell lines, and imaging conditions. Aligning microscopy images with biological concepts in natural language is challenging because most images lack descriptive captions and biological knowledge annotations. We therefore progressively establish image-text associations at three levels to provide MicroCLIP with richer semantic supervision: (1) category-level alignment links images to standardized labels defined by subcellular structure-cell-line combinations; (2) knowledge-level alignment incorporates biological priors derived from these labels to capture relationships across species and cell types; and (3) detail-level alignment uses a multimodal large language model to recaption and verify image-text pairs at scale, emphasizing fine-grained visual characteristics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MicroCLIP achieves leading performance in zero-shot classification and text-image retrieval on microscopy images. It retrieves images with specific cellular characteristics from thousands of candidates and generalizes to unseen biological concepts without additional supervision. These results highlight MicroCLIP's potential as a foundation model for fluorescence microscopy.

</details>


### [286] [LoopREG: Representation Supervision Enables Test-Time Recurrent Depth in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17877)
*Zipeng Wang,Anping Xiong,Jing Zhang,Xiaoya Zhu,Yi Jiang,Yanbing Xiao,Zige Zeng,Zezhou Chen,Xiang Liu,Huan Hu,Ping Chen,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Ye LV,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Zhengrui Huang,Bo Shen,Yuren You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopREG 提出一种表示监督的循环扩散 Transformer，通过共享块反复作用于受预训练视觉编码器监督的状态，将训练深度学得的循环算子外推到更多推理访问次数，从而在推理时近似无损地加深生成计算；在 ImageNet 256×256 上显著降低 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用 Transformer 块可在测试时增加扩散模型的有效计算量，但朴素循环会导致生成质量退化，超出其训练深度。作者希望找到一种使循环深度可外推、从而在推理阶段安全增加计算量的机制。

Method: 提出 LoopREG：在循环扩散 Transformer 中反复应用同一共享块，并对最后一个循环访问后的状态施加预训练视觉编码器（REPA 类）表示对齐监督。训练完成后，推理时将该学得的循环算子扩展到更多访问次数，权重不变；并在 SiT-B/2、SiT-L/2、SiT-XL/2 上及多种对照干预下验证监督位置的影响。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，LoopREG 将 SiT-B/2 的 FID 从局部 REG 基线的 15.08 降至 4.91（400K 步），降幅 67.41%。在两访问训练的检查点内，额外一次推理访问可在权重不变下将 FID 从 14.71 改善到 5.34。增益在 SiT-L/2 与 SiT-XL/2 上持续存在。受控实验显示有用循环受益于表示监督的位置，而 REPA 对齐带来的外推增益较小。

Conclusion: 表示监督的循环是一种通过增加推理深度提升扩散生成质量的实用路径。

Abstract: Reusing Transformer blocks offers a way to increase diffusion-model computation at test time, but naive recurrence can degrade generation beyond its training depth. We introduce LoopREG, a representation-supervised recurrent diffusion Transformer that repeatedly applies a shared block at the state supervised by a pretrained visual encoder. Alignment is applied after the final recurrent visit during training; inference extends the same learned operator to additional visits. On ImageNet 256 x 256, LoopREG reduces SiT-B/2 FID from the local REG baseline's 15.08 to 4.91 at 400K training steps, a 67.41% reduction. Within a checkpoint trained with two visits, one extra inference visit improves FID from 14.71 to 5.34 with unchanged weights. The benefit persists on SiT-L/2 and SiT-XL/2. Controlled interventions show that useful recurrence follows the location of representation supervision, while REPA alignment produces a smaller extrapolation benefit. These results make representation-supervised recurrence a practical route to improving diffusion generation through additional inference depth.

</details>


### [287] [Diagnose-to-Generate: Eliminating Structural Distortions in Flow Models via Patch-Level Grounded Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17882)
*Yifan Li,Lishuai Gao,Tong Zhang,Qilong Huang,Chen Zhao,Zhuoliang Kang,Jie Hu,Yujie Zhong,Fenghe Tang,S Kevin Zhou,Xiaoming Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diagnose-to-Generate (D2G) 框架，将视觉诊断与生成优化解耦：先构建区域标注数据集 Achilles-600K 并训练 ARGUS 诊断模型预测结构异常类别与边界框，再将其转化为置信度加权空间奖励用于 Patch-level Flow-GRPO，并通过 Trajectory-Preserving Stochastic Sampling (TPSS) 缓解组内空间漂移。在 FLUX.1-dev 与 SD3.5 上提升了结构保真度，同时保持 FID 与 CLIP 分数，并在多种第三方评估下表现一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式图像生成器虽具备强语义合成能力，仍会产生局部结构畸变（如手部与四肢畸形）。现有后训练方法多优化全局标量奖励，导致空间信用分配薄弱，可能在修复局部缺陷时破坏原本完好的区域。因此需要一种能够精准定位缺陷并进行局部优化的对齐框架。

Method: 1) 构建区域标注的结构异常数据集 Achilles-600K；2) 训练 ARGUS 诊断模型，采用监督冷启动与 Region-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization，输出异常类别与边界框；3) 将诊断结果转化为置信度加权的空间奖励，用于 Patch-level Flow-GRPO；4) 提出 Trajectory-Preserving Stochastic Sampling (TPSS)，在前段去噪使用确定性 ODE 轨迹、仅在后期引入随机性，以限制组内空间漂移并保留局部探索。

Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev 与 SD3.5 上，方法提升了结构保真度，同时保持 FID 与 CLIP 分数。在 HumanRefiner、零样本 GPT-4.1 以及盲测人类评估下均取得一致增益，表明改进不局限于训练奖励模型。

Conclusion: 将视觉诊断与生成优化分离、并以区域级空间奖励驱动流模型后训练，可有效纠正局部结构畸变而不损害全局语义质量，且泛化性优于单一奖励模型优化。

Abstract: Flow-based image generators achieve strong semantic synthesis but still produce localized structural distortions, such as malformed hands and limbs. Existing post-training methods typically optimize global scalar rewards, which provide weak spatial credit assignment and may alter well-formed regions while correcting local defects. We present \textit{Diagnose-to-Generate (D2G)}, a grounded alignment framework that separates visual diagnosis from generative optimization. We construct \textbf{Achilles-600K}, a region-annotated dataset for structural anomalies, and train \textbf{ARGUS} through supervised cold-start and Region-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization to predict anomaly categories and bounding boxes. These diagnoses are converted into confidence-weighted spatial rewards for our \textbf{Patch-level Flow-GRPO}. We further identify that global stochastic exploration can shift semantic regions across group rollouts, making patch-wise relative advantages spatially inconsistent. Our \textbf{Trajectory-Preserving Stochastic Sampling (TPSS)} anchors early denoising with deterministic ODE trajectories and introduces stochasticity only at later steps, thereby limiting intra-group spatial drift while retaining local exploration. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and SD3.5 show improved structural fidelity with preserved FID and CLIP scores. Consistent gains under HumanRefiner, zero-shot GPT-4.1, and blinded human evaluation further demonstrate that the improvements extend beyond the training reward model.

</details>


### [288] [Visual Dependence Does Not Imply Answerability: A Systematic Analysis of Answerability Signals in Frozen InstructBLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17902)
*Minjun Kim,Hyunmin Song,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了冻结InstructBLIP在UNK-VQA子集上的多种可回答性信号，发现所有信号判别能力均很弱（AUROC约0.50-0.54），无法作为可靠的可回答性代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉问答模型在视觉证据不足时仍会生成看似合理的回答。尽管已有基准和弃答机制，但在冻结视觉语言模型上，哪些信号能可靠指示未见图像-问题对的可回答性仍不明确，因此需要系统评估。

Method: 在UNK-VQA的分层10K子集上，使用冻结的InstructBLIP，涵盖预测不确定性、跨模态对齐、视觉定位、视觉依赖性、内部表示和学习探针六类可回答性信号，并开展配对bootstrap分析、针对性视觉证据移除干预、探针容量与信号组合实验、冻结后交叉验证及选择性回答与失败模式分析。

Result: 可回答性判别普遍很弱，测试AUROC大多在0.50-0.54。CLIP对齐点估计最高，但在配对bootstrap下未显著优于生成置信度。针对性移除问题相关视觉证据引起比随机掩码大得多的置信度变化，但干预敏感性几乎无判别力。增加探针容量、组合信号、加入推理感知监督均无稳健提升，冻结后交叉验证同样弱。选择性回答在低风险准则下可用覆盖率有限，各信号相对效用随扰动机制变化。

Conclusion: 在冻结InstructBLIP的UNK-VQA子集上，视觉依赖性、不确定性、语义对齐和静态表示都不是稳健的独立可回答性代理指标。研究强调需区分模型对视觉证据的依赖与证据本身是否足以支持回答。

Abstract: Visual question answering models often produce plausible responses even when the available visual evidence is insufficient to support a valid answer. Although prior work has introduced benchmarks and abstention mechanisms for unanswerable visual questions, it remains unclear which signals derived from a frozen vision-language model reliably indicate answerability on unseen image-question pairs. We systematically evaluate answerability signals on a stratified 10K subset of UNK-VQA using frozen InstructBLIP, covering predictive uncertainty, cross-modal alignment, visual grounding, visual dependence, internal representations, and learned probes. Answerability discrimination remains weak, with test AUROC largely confined to 0.50-0.54. CLIP alignment yields the highest point estimate but does not significantly outperform generation confidence under paired bootstrap analysis. Targeted removal of question-relevant visual evidence causes substantially larger changes in answer confidence than same-size random masking, yet intervention sensitivity provides essentially no answerability discrimination. Increasing probe capacity, combining multiple signals, and adding reason-aware supervision do not provide robust improvements, while post-freeze cross-validation yields similarly weak discrimination. Selective-answering analysis shows limited usable coverage under low-risk operating criteria. Failure-mode analysis shows that the relative utility of individual signals varies across perturbation mechanisms. Taken together, these results show that, on our UNK-VQA subset with frozen InstructBLIP, visual dependence, uncertainty, semantic alignment, and static representations are not robust standalone proxies for answerability. Our findings emphasize the need to distinguish a model's reliance on visual evidence from whether that evidence is sufficient to support an answer.

</details>


### [289] [DriveWLA: Unifying Future Dynamics and Scene Semantics through World-Language-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17903)
*Shuzheng Wang,Haoxuan Li,Shengzhen Li,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveWLA，一个基于Mixture-of-Transformers的统一World-Language-Action模型，融合语义理解、未来场景演化与轨迹生成，并通过Flow-GRPO强化学习后训练提升动作专家，在开环与闭环评测中取得领先性能并展现零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）擅长语义理解，世界模型擅长未来场景演化，但将二者能力有效整合以生成轨迹仍具挑战。本文旨在统一建模语义理解、场景动态与轨迹生成，突破现有方法在融合上的局限。

Method: 提出DriveWLA，基于Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，包含三个专家：World Expert（未来预测）、Language Expert（语义理解）、Action Expert（轨迹生成）。引入Cross-Expert Attention实现跨专家的可控信息流，并对齐语义与动态表征；同时采用Flow-GRPO强化学习后训练来精炼Action Expert，以超越模仿学习。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到92.3 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到90.1 EPDMS，在基于VLM和世界模型的规划器中表现领先；无需特定微调即可零样本泛化到HUGSIM、nuScenes和Bench2Drive。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了统一World-Language-Action建模的有效性，DriveWLA可作为自动驾驶的可泛化框架，兼具开环与闭环评测的优越性能与跨数据集零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and world models have demonstrated strong capabilities in semantic understanding and future scene evolution for autonomous driving. However, effectively integrating both capabilities for trajectory generation remains challenging. In this work, we propose DriveWLA, a unified World-Language-Action model based on Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) that jointly models semantic understanding, future scene dynamics, and trajectory generation. DriveWLA consists of three experts: a World Expert for future prediction, a Language Expert for semantic understanding, and an Action Expert for trajectory generation. We further introduce Cross-Expert Attention, which enables controlled information flow across experts while aligning semantic and dynamic representations. To improve trajectory generation beyond imitation learning, we apply Flow-GRPO reinforcement learning post-training to refine the Action Expert. Extensive experiments show that DriveWLA achieves superior performance in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluation. DriveWLA achieves 92.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, demonstrating strong performance among VLM-based and world-model-based planners. Without specific fine-tuning, DriveWLA further demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to HUGSIM, nuScenes, and Bench2Drive. These results validate the effectiveness of our unified World-Language-Action modeling, establishing DriveWLA as a generalizable framework for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [290] [LinMorph: Learning to Morph Weights for Efficient Video DiT Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17914)
*Lingyi Long,Congyue Zhao,Xiaoou Li,Yanmin Qian,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinMorph将Video DiT线性化重构为可学习的softmax到线性权重变换问题，通过学习跨参数morphism实现预训练知识的高效迁移，显著加快恢复速度并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video DiT后置线性化方法主要关注注意力替换带来的质量损失恢复问题，但本文指出更根本的瓶颈在上游初始化：随机初始化丢弃预训练知识，而人工权重复用依赖僵硬且不完整的源-目标对应关系。

Method: 提出LinMorph，学习一个跨参数morphism，使每个目标Gated DeltaNet参数能联合从预训练query、key、value和output投影中抽取，包括无直接softmax对应参数的参数。单个morphism跨层共享，通过局部teacher对齐学习，同时依据各层预训练权重产生层特定初始化。所得权重物化为独立GDN模块，用于渐进蒸馏和联合恢复。由于morphism在权重空间而非token空间操作，可在短低分辨率片段上学习并直接复用于更大视频尺度。

Result: 在多个预训练Video DiT上，LinMorph相比随机初始化和启发式权重复用，实现明显更快的恢复速度和更好的最终生成质量。

Conclusion: 有效的线性化不仅取决于转换后模型如何恢复，更关键取决于恢复前预训练知识如何迁移。LinMorph表明初始化是Video DiT线性化的根本瓶颈，权重空间的可学习变换能有效解决该问题。

Abstract: Post-hoc linearization is an attractive way to accelerate pretrained Video Diffusion Transformers (Video DiTs), yet existing methods largely treat the quality loss induced by attention replacement as a recovery problem. We show that a more fundamental bottleneck lies upstream: \emph{initialization}. Random initialization discards pretrained knowledge, whereas manual weight reuse transfers it through rigid and incomplete source--target correspondences. We introduce \textbf{LinMorph}, which recasts Video DiT linearization as a learnable softmax-to-linear weight transformation problem. Instead of copying predefined counterparts, LinMorph learns a cross-parameter morphism that lets each target Gated DeltaNet (GDN) parameter draw jointly from pretrained query, key, value, and output projections, including for parameters with no direct softmax counterpart. A single morphism is shared across layers and learned through local teacher alignment, while producing layer-specific initializations from their respective pretrained weights. The resulting weights are then materialized as independent GDN modules for progressive distillation and joint recovery.Since the morphism operates in weight space rather than token space, it can be learned on short, low-resolution clips and directly reused at larger video scales. Across pretrained Video DiTs, LinMorph enables markedly faster recovery and better final generation quality than random initialization and heuristic weight reuse. Results show that efficient linearization depends not only on how a converted model is recovered, but critically on how pretrained knowledge is transferred before recovery. Project page: https://lonelyling.github.io/LinMorph.

</details>


### [291] [CineMem: Attention-Aligned Fine-grained Memory Retrieval for Multi-Shot Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17926)
*Xiaoxue Wu,Xinyu Gao,Fang Yu,Yu Qiao,Xinyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CineMem，一种面向自回归多镜头视频生成的注意力对齐细粒度记忆检索框架，通过在线聚类KV缓存与查询级自适应检索，在固定记忆预算下逼近全记忆访问，提升跨镜头长程一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成依赖记忆检索建模长程依赖，多镜头生成中需在镜头切换带来的显著视觉变化与语义连续性之间取得平衡。现有方法在帧级别进行记忆检索，并对所有查询token共享同一历史上下文，忽视了异构信息需求，导致检索冗余与历史记忆利用低效。

Method: 受注意力分析揭示的内在记忆访问模式启发，提出online-clustered KV cache作为记忆组织策略，对全部历史token进行语义感知分组以实现更精确检索；同时引入查询级检索机制，在固定检索预算下提供更广的历史信息访问。

Result: 该设计使多镜头视频生成器在镜头切换及重访场景中保持长程一致性，并在受限记忆预算下逼近全记忆访问性能。同时提出一套任务特定、VLM辅助的指标用于忠实评估镜头间一致性。大量实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CineMem通过细粒度、查询自适应的记忆检索组织，有效缓解了多镜头自回归视频生成中的冗余检索与记忆利用低效问题，并配套提出评估指标，实验证明其有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation relies on memory retrieval to model long-range dependencies. This capability becomes particularly critical for multi-shot generation, where models must reconcile substantial visual changes across shot transitions with persistent semantic continuity. Existing methods tend to perform memory retrieval at the frame level and share the same historical context across all query tokens, overlooking the heterogeneous information needs and leading to redundant retrieval and inefficient use of historical memory. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineMem, an attention-aligned fine-grained memory retrieval framework that combines online memory organization with query-adaptive retrieval for autoregressive multi-shot video generation. Specifically, guided by intrinsic memory-access patterns revealed through attention analysis, we propose an online-clustered KV cache as a memory organization strategy over all historical tokens, enabling semantic-aware memory grouping and more precise retrieval. In addition, we introduce a query-level retrieval mechanism that provides broader access to historical information under a fixed retrieval budget, thereby improving long-term memory utilization. Together, these designs enable the multi-shot video generator to preserve long-range consistency across shot transitions, particularly in revisitation scenarios, while approximating full-memory access under a constrained memory budget. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce a suite of task-specific, VLM-assisted metrics for faithful inter-shot consistency assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [292] [HierarchiRetina: A Diabetic Retinopathy Screening and Grading Cascade with Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17933)
*Md Asif Uddin,Niloy Farhan,Rafeed Rahman,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HierarchiRetina分阶段筛查-分割-分级级联系统，通过异构专家分割、可分级性分离与病变掩膜交叉注意力、端到端评估，在不丢弃不可分级图像的同时实现可追溯的糖网筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床糖网筛查本身是分阶段的，但多数自动化系统用单一黑盒网络直接对眼底图分级，存在三个缺口：(i) 病变在尺寸与对比度上差异大，分割器却对所有病变使用相同模块；(ii) 不可分级图像常被剔除或当作额外类别，且病变感知的分级器很少检验病变是否真正驱动分级；(iii) 分阶段系统通常只在到达该阶段的图像上报告指标，导致误差传播未被测量。

Method: HierarchiRetina包含三个可检查的阶段输出。其一，HSMoE-AUNet：U-Net的跳连、瓶颈与解码块均替换为异构专家的稀疏混合（MoE），并按病变匹配专家类型、数量与损失函数；SwihHRUNetPP为双编码器血管分割器，采用视野受限损失。其二，LG-DRG将可分级性与序数严重度解耦，并让病变掩膜通过门控交叉注意力查询图像特征，从而检验掩膜对分级的影响。其三，端到端评估将筛查漏检计为错误，并把每个错误归因到具体阶段。

Result: 汇总Dice从0.184（微动脉瘤）到0.579（渗出）；血管Dice为0.731。在来自六个数据集的58,689张留出图像上，端到端二次加权kappa为0.804，转诊敏感度0.832（特异度0.947），筛查门控贡献了66%的错误。消融实验显示，即使交叉注意力门初始为开启状态，分级仍忽略掩膜。在全部4,105张官方DDR测试图像上，六分类准确率85.6%，高于所检索到的最佳已发表六分类结果（83.8%）。

Conclusion: 分阶段筛查系统在临床部署前应进行端到端评估，并配合阶段误差归因与掩膜消融，以真实反映系统可靠性与误差来源；可检查的中间输出与解耦设计有助于暴露被单阶段指标掩盖的问题。

Abstract: Clinical screening for diabetic retinopathy proceeds in stages, yet most automated systems grade fundus images with a single black-box network. Three gaps remain: (i) lesions differ widely in size and contrast, but segmenters typically apply the same blocks to all; (ii) ungradable images are usually excluded or treated as an extra class, and lesion-aware graders seldom test whether lesions drive the grade; (iii) staged systems often report each stage only on the images reaching it, leaving propagated errors unmeasured. HierarchiRetina, a screening, segmentation and grading cascade with inspectable stage outputs, addresses each gap. First, HSMoE-AUNet is a U-Net whose skip, bottleneck and decoder blocks are sparse mixtures of heterogeneous experts, with lesion-matched types, counts and losses; SwinHRUNetPP, a dual-encoder vessel segmenter, uses a field-of-view-restricted loss. Second, LG-DRG separates gradability from ordinal severity and lets lesion masks query the image through gated cross-attention. Third, an end-to-end evaluation counts screening misses as errors and traces each error to its stage. Pooled Dice ranged from 0.184 (microaneurysms) to 0.579 (exudates); vessel Dice was 0.731. On 58,689 held-out images from six datasets, end-to-end quadratic weighted kappa was 0.804, with referral sensitivity 0.832 at specificity 0.947, and the screening gate caused 66% of errors. In ablations, the grade ignored the masks, even with the cross-attention gate started open. On all 4,105 official DDR test images, six-class accuracy was 85.6%, above the best published six-class result we found (83.8%). Staged screening systems should be evaluated end-to-end, with stage attribution and mask ablation, before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [293] [PUGS: Physics-Guided Uncertainty Modeling for Underwater Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17936)
*Tingjun Li,Yuxi Wang,Guoqiang Zhong,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PUGS，一种面向水下动态3DGS的物理引导不确定性建模框架。它将每个高斯运动的确定性位移替换为含期望形变与物理不确定性的概率分布，通过随机形变优化、不确定性随机退火与双路径自一致性蒸馏，实现从随机探索到确定性重建的转换，在UVEB与DRUVA上分别取得1.16 dB和3.99 dB的PSNR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法多以确定性形变场建模场景动态，假设每个高斯沿确定几何轨迹运动。该假设在水下失效：光散射、折射、海雪与低能见度使图像存在显著物理歧义，导致轨迹估计不可靠，重建质量与时序一致性下降。因此需要将水下成像形成过程中固有的不确定性显式建模，并作为物理信号指导动态场景建模。

Method: PUGS将每个高斯运动参数化为概率分布，包含期望形变与物理不确定性；采用基于局部重参数化的随机形变优化采样高斯轨迹，在训练中探索多个物理合理的解；进一步引入不确定性随机退火与双路径自一致性蒸馏目标，将随机探索逐步转化为确定性重建，同时保持时序相干性。

Result: 在水下动态数据集与物理感知基准上的大量实验表明，PUGS在渲染质量、时序一致性与重建鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。相比最强基线，在UVEB上PSNR提升1.16 dB，在DRUVA上提升3.99 dB。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模水下成像诱导的固有不确定性并将其作为物理先验信号，PUGS有效缓解了确定性形变假设在水下场景中的失效问题，验证了不确定性建模在动态3DGS中的有效性，为水下动态场景重建提供了新的物理引导范式。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient framework for dynamic scene reconstruction. Most existing approaches model scene dynamics with deterministic deformation fields, assuming each Gaussian follows a distinct geometric trajectory over time. This assumption breaks down underwater, where light scattering, refraction, marine snow and low visibility create substantial physical ambiguity in captured images. We present PUGS, a physics-guided uncertainty model for underwater dynamic 3DGS. PUGS learns inherent uncertainty induced by underwater image formation and leverages it as a physical signal for dynamic scene modeling. Rather than estimating a single deterministic displacement, it parameterizes each Gaussian's motion as a probability distribution with expected deformation and physical uncertainty. We adopt stochastic deformation optimization to sample Gaussian trajectories via local reparameterization, exploring multiple physically plausible solutions during training. We further propose uncertainty stochastic annealing and a dual-path self-consistency distillation objective, converting stochastic exploration to deterministic reconstruction while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive experiments on underwater dynamic datasets and physics-aware benchmarks demonstrate superior rendering quality, temporal consistency and reconstruction robustness. Our method achieves 1.16 dB PSNR gain over the strongest baseline on UVEB and 3.99 dB on DRUVA.

</details>


### [294] [MERIT: Multimodal Evidence Reasoning over Image Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17937)
*Haoyang Jiang,Zhuotong Fan,Mingyang Yi,Ju Fan,Lanqing Hu,Xiao Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MERIT三阶段框架，直接在表格图像上进行多模态证据推理，串行化表格作为可选辅助输入，通过编码器适配、监督微调与强化学习提升表格问答与事实验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格推理通常假设可获取干净的文本表格，但实际中表格多以文本序列化形式呈现，会破坏单元格内容、扭曲结构关系并丢失视觉线索，导致模型基于不完整证据推理。而表格图像保留了这些信息，但利用图像需要准确的感知与基于相关单元格的推理。

Method: 提出MERIT（Multimodal Evidence Reasoning over Image Tables）三阶段框架：1）视觉证据获取瓶颈驱动表格专用编码器适配，采用重建、定位与颜色条件监督；2）基于适配表示进行监督微调，利用验证过的轨迹教授显式证据选择与推理；3）强化学习优化策略，对证据正确性、计算、最终答案与输出格式分别设置奖励，以对中间步骤提供反馈。

Result: 在表格问答与事实验证任务上进行评估；分析视觉输入与各训练组件的贡献；并考察对不同表格结构与视觉呈现的额外数据集的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MERIT通过图像表格证据推理与分步奖励反馈，可针对最终答案监督无法区分的检索与计算错误进行优化，并在多类表格任务上展现有效性。

Abstract: Table reasoning with language models typically assumes access to clean textual tables. In practice, tables are often serialized into text, which can corrupt cell contents, distort structural relationships, and discard visual cues, leaving the model to reason over incomplete evidence. In contrast, table images retain this information, but using it requires both accurate perception and reasoning grounded in the relevant cells. To address this, we propose MERIT (Multimodal Evidence Reasoning over Image Tables), a three-stage framework for learning to reason over table images, with serialized tables serving as optional auxiliary inputs (if provided). Our analysis identifies visual evidence acquisition as a bottleneck, motivating table-specific encoder adaptation through reconstruction, localization, and color-conditioned supervision. Building on the adapted representations, supervised fine-tuning teaches explicit evidence selection and reasoning using verified trajectories. Finally, reinforcement learning refines the policy with separate rewards for evidence correctness, calculation, final answers, and output format. By providing feedback on intermediate steps, the framework targets retrieval and computational errors that final-answer supervision alone does not distinguish. We evaluate the approach on table question answering and fact verification, analyze the contributions of visual inputs and training components, and assess generalization to additional datasets with different table structures and visual presentations.

</details>


### [295] [AI-Enhanced Hyperspectral Imaging for Intraoperative Detection of Low-Grade Glioma: A Clinical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17944)
*Siri Willems,Ishika Jain,Jens De Winne,Siri Luthman,Hamed Zivari Adab,Roeland Vandebriel,Anna Alperovich,Xiaohan Zhang,Ben Stoffelen,Tom De Schepper,Lien Solie,Steven De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对低级别胶质瘤(LGG)，利用术中高光谱成像(HSI)刻画健康与肿瘤组织的谱差异，并在配对组织位置、相同患者级数据划分下对比HSI与RGB分类性能，3D CNN取得最高中位ROC-AUC 74.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像引导神经导航技术（术中MRI、超声）成本高、灵敏度有限且操作复杂，需要更高效的系统提升术中肿瘤检测与切除效果；既往术中脑HSI研究多关注高级别胶质瘤或混合级别队列，而LGG在白光显微镜下与正常脑组织高度相似，缺乏专门的谱特征刻画与与RGB的严格对照比较。

Method: 临床术中HSI研究，聚焦低级别胶质瘤；刻画健康组织与肿瘤组织的谱差异；在配对的匹配组织位置、相同患者级数据划分条件下，进行HSI与RGB分类的配对比较；评估多种分类模型，包括融合谱与空间特征的3D CNN、仅谱特征的XGBoost，以及基于RGB的模型，以ROC-AUC为指标。

Result: 肿瘤区域反射率一致较低，可能与血红蛋白浓度和氧合差异相关。3D CNN取得最高中位ROC-AUC 74.5%，仅谱特征的XGBoost为67.9%，基于RGB的模型为64.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持进一步研究HSI用于低级别胶质瘤分类，并在该队列中量化了HSI相对传统RGB成像的性能优势。

Abstract: Current image-guided neuronavigation techniques, such as intraoperative MRI and ultrasound, are limited by high costs, limited sensitivity and operational complexity. There is a clear need for more efficient systems to enhance intra-operative tumor detection and improve patient outcomes for intrinsic brain tumor resection. Whereas previous intraoperative brain HSI studies have predominantly investigated high-grade gliomas, glioblastomas, or mixed-grade cohorts, this clinical study focuses specifically on low-grade gliomas (LGGs). LGGs strongly resemble normal brain under white light conditions on the operative microscope. This study characterizes spectral differences between healthy and tumor tissue and provides a paired comparison of HSI- and RGB-based classification at matched tissue locations and under identical patient-level data splits. Tumor regions showed consistently lower reflectance, which may be associated with differences in hemoglobin concentration and oxygenation. A range of classification models was evaluated. The 3D CNN, which integrates spectral and spatial features, achieved the highest median ROC-AUC of 74.5%, compared with 67.9% for the spectral-only XGBoost model and 64.0% for the RGB-based model. These results support further investigation of HSI for low-grade glioma classification and quantify its performance relative to conventional RGB imaging in this cohort.

</details>


### [296] [FETALFusion: Resolution-Aware State-Space Modeling for Multi-Domain Fetal Head Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17951)
*Ammar Bhilwarawala,Mainak Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FETALFusion——一种双路径 CNN-Mamba 编码器-解码器，用于跨采集方式的胎儿头部超声分割，并对其增益来源进行了严格消融与校准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头围（HC）是胎儿生长的常规超声标志物，其自动化依赖分割边界在单张扫描上稳健，而非仅平均表现良好。现有方法在跨采集方式（经腹与经会阴）场景下的稳定性与增益归因尚不明确。

Method: 模型为双路径 CNN-Mamba 编码器-解码器：仅在两个最高分辨率阶段施加四方向状态空间扫描；瓶颈特征经域特定通道门控，测试时取平均；引入弱关键点形状先验。联合在经腹 HC-18 与经会阴 PSFHS 上训练，在 471 张留出测试集上以原生分辨率表面距离评价十种方法，统一训练调度，无测试时增强、无域标签。

Result: FETALFusion 达到 Dice 0.963、HD95 2.10 mm；在全部五项指标上优于 U-Mamba（HD95 降低 0.31 mm，Holm 校正 p < 10⁻⁴），该优势在测试时增强与两次独立训练下保持，并在均值上优于 U-Net、Attention U-Net 与 TransUNet。ResNet-34 UNet++ 超过 FETALFusion，ImageNet 初始化 ResNet-34 U-Net 最佳（HD95 1.75 mm）。消融显示可测增益主要来自关键点形状先验，在经会阴图像上来自域门控，移除状态空间分支未带来可测精度变化。预测掩膜所得 HC 与临床参考的组内相关系数为 0.9986。

Conclusion: 自动化胎儿头部分割的增益更依赖强残差编码器与形状先验，而非状态空间分支；所提方法在无域标签条件下仍具竞争力，且由掩膜导出的头围与临床参考高度一致，具备潜在的临床测量价值。

Abstract: Head circumference (HC) measured from 2D ultrasound is a routine marker of fetal growth, and automating it needs segmentations whose boundaries hold up on individual scans, not only on average. We present FETALFusion, a dual-path CNN-Mamba encoder-decoder for fetal head segmentation across acquisition types. It applies four-directional state-space scans only at the two highest-resolution stages, routes bottleneck features through domain-specific channel gates that are averaged at test time, and adds a weak landmark shape prior. We train jointly on transabdominal (HC-18) and transperineal (PSFHS) images and compare ten methods on a held-out test set of 471 images, each trained under one schedule and scored by surface distances at native resolution. FETALFusion reaches a Dice score of 0.963 and an HD95 of 2.10 mm without test-time augmentation or a domain label. It improves on U-Mamba on all five reported metrics (HD95 reduced by 0.31 mm; Holm-corrected ($p < 10^{-4}$), a lead that holds under test-time augmentation and across two independent training runs, and in the mean on U-Net, Attention U-Net and TransUNet. Residual encoders close the gap: a ResNet-34 UNet++ exceeds FETALFusion, and an ImageNet initialised ResNet-34 U-Net is best overall (HD95 1.75 mm). The ablation agrees. Calibrated against run-to-run variation, it attributes FETALFusion's measurable gains to the landmark shape prior and, on transperineal images, to the domain gates, while removing the state-space branch does not measurably change accuracy. HC from the predicted masks agrees with the clinical reference at an intraclass correlation of 0.9986.

</details>


### [297] [dMRI-F: A Foundation Model for Generalizable Diffusion MRI Analysis from Sparse Acquisitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17968)
*Xi Zhu,Yijie Li,Xiaofan Wang,Wei Zhang,Huafu Chen,Ce Zhu,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 dMRI-F，一个扩散磁共振基础模型，通过 q-space 掩码重建预训练，仅需 6 个体积即可统一数据增强与多种下游任务，并在 9 个数据集上取得优于现有方法的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量 dMRI 分析依赖密集采样，导致扫描时间长、硬件要求高，实际常不可行；且 dMRI 需要专门计算方法支持分割、配准、合成等多种任务。因此亟需一个能在稀疏采集输入下统一增强与下游任务的通用框架。

Method: 提出基础模型 dMRI-F。预训练任务为 q-space 掩码重建：仅用 6 个体积预测密集采样的多壳数据，从而学习可泛化的 dMRI 潜特征表示；该表示作为核心基础，适配脑区分割、脑配准与跨模态合成等多样下游任务。

Result: 在 9 个独立采集的数据集上，dMRI-F 在采集方式、人群和任务上均优于现有最先进方法，表现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: dMRI-F 具备通用性与灵活性，可从低成本、稀疏采集实现多样 dMRI 应用，是一个通用框架。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) probes water diffusion within the brain, providing unique information about tissue microstructure and white matter connectivity. High-quality dMRI enables advanced and accurate analyses but demands dense acquisitions, resulting in long scan times and hardware constraints that are often infeasible. Further, dMRI requires dedicated computational methods to support diverse analysis tasks (e.g., parcellation, registration, and synthesis) for both neuroscientific and clinical applications. Here, we present dMRI-F, a foundation model that unifies dMRI data enhancement and downstream tasks across diverse acquisitions and populations, while requiring only minimal acquisition input. dMRI-F is pre-trained with a q-space masked reconstruction task: predicting densely sampled multi-shell data from only six volumes. It learns generalizable latent feature representations of dMRI data, which serve as a core foundation for adapting to diverse downstream tasks, including brain parcellation, brain registration, and cross-modal synthesis. Across nine independently acquired datasets, we demonstrate that dMRI-F outperforms state-of-the-art methods with robust generalization across acquisitions, populations, and tasks. Our results demonstrate dMRI-F's versatility as a general-purpose framework that enables diverse dMRI applications from low-cost, sparse acquisitions.

</details>


### [298] [Web Scale on a Budget: Tail-Aware Retrieval Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17986)
*Boaz Meivar,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逐查询检索难度（per-query retrieval hardness）的评估缩放律，证明用小规模随机子集即可准确估计大规模检索基准上的mAP，从而避免昂贵的全规模索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例检索基准（如ILIAS）通过扩充图库规模（可达1亿张干扰图）来提升判别能力，但全量索引代价高昂（数百GPU小时、每模型约200GB描述符），且每个方法或消融实验都需重复支付。作者质疑"忠实评估必须依赖web规模索引"这一普遍假设。

Method: 作者提出评估本身遵循缩放律：超越正样本的干扰图数量服从图库规模的二项分布。据此定义与图库规模无关的"逐查询检索难度"量，并证明可从小的随机样本中估计该难度，进而在任意规模上生成检索指标。该方法将推荐系统与生物特征评估中已有的采样秩机制首次适配到视觉检索，并使其逐查询化、可估计化。

Result: 在ILIAS、INSTRE（100M规模）和GLDv2（759k全部干扰池）上验证：仅用5%样本（索引量减少20倍），五种骨干网络的尾部感知（EVT）估计即收敛到大规模mAP，误差约0.01（平均绝对误差0.005）；推荐系统计数基线则普遍高估（+0.037至+0.068）；而直接在子集上朴素报告指标误差为+0.08至+0.13。

Conclusion: 该评估缩放律能显著降低检索评估的索引成本与能耗，实现更快、更省、更绿色的评估，作者开源了代码及逐查询难度数据以促进该方法的推广。

Abstract: A common practice in instance retrieval is to grow the gallery size to improve a benchmark's discrimination capability. One prominent example is ILIAS, which adds up to 100 million distractor images. The prevailing assumption is that faithful evaluation demands web scale, and that indexing all 100M images through the backbone is the unavoidable price of a comparable number. That price is steep: hundreds of GPU-hours and ~200 GB of descriptors per model, paid again for every method or ablation. We show the web-scale indexing price is largely avoidable: evaluation itself obeys a scaling law. The number of distractors that outrank a positive is Binomial in the gallery size. We define the per-query retrieval hardness, a gallery-scale agnostic quantity that yields a retrieval metric at any scale, and demonstrate it can be derived from a small random sample. The underlying sampled-rank machinery is long established in recommender-system and biometric evaluation, but to our knowledge has never been adapted to visual retrieval. We make the law per-query and estimable, and validate it at true 100M scale on ILIAS and INSTRE, and on the full 759k-distractor Google Landmarks v2 pool. Concretely, within a 5% sample (a flat 20x less indexing), every backbone's tail-aware (EVT) estimate has converged onto the large-scale mAP, to within ~0.01 (mean absolute error 0.005), across five backbones, three benchmarks, and two distractor pools (mAP@1000 at 100M on ILIAS/INSTRE; the official mAP@100 at the full 759k pool on GLDv2); the recommender-systems count baseline over-predicts everywhere (+0.037 to +0.068). This is an order of magnitude better than naively reporting the metric on the same subset (+0.08 to +0.13). We release code and the per-query hardness to facilitate faster, cheaper, and greener retrieval evaluation.

</details>


### [299] [PolUR: Polarization Aware Multimodal Degradation Perception for Underwater Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17991)
*Wenshuo Zhang,Xiangyue Zhang,Junlin Li,Wenjie Tian,Yubo Zheng,Hongli Xu,Jingyu Ru,Xinde Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PolUR——一个融合偏振与RGB信息的多模态语言驱动框架，通过结构化语义指令识别退化类型与程度，并在退化感知语义特征引导下增强联合复原表征；同时构建MixPolar基准，实验证明其在退化感知、图像复原及下游任务上具有竞争力与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下退化图像的高质量复原本对水下探测至关重要。偏振观测在清水条件下可为恢复目标辐亮度提供互补物理线索，但现有方法未能联合建模原始强度信息与偏振线索，难以在理解混合退化（色偏、雾化、低光）的基础上驱动图像复原。

Method: 提出PolUR统一多模态语言驱动框架：联合利用偏振与RGB信息，通过结构化语义指令识别退化类型及严重程度；并以退化感知语义特征为引导，增强联合复原表征，从而提升混合退化下的复原鲁棒性。同时构建MixPolar基准，包含具有真实世界退化的水下图像及其退化描述，支持退化感知、图像复原与下游任务的系统性评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，PolUR能有效整合偏振与RGB信息，在退化感知和图像复原方面取得有竞争力的性能，并展现出泛化能力以及对下游任务的实用性。

Conclusion: PolUR通过语言驱动的多模态联合建模，将退化理解与图像复原统一起来，配合MixPolar基准，为混合退化水下图像复原提供了有效方案并支持下游应用。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality images from degraded underwater observations is crucial for underwater exploration. Polarization observations show promise as an effective source of complementary physical cues for recovering target radiance under clear-water conditions. However, existing methods have yet to jointly model raw intensity information and polarization cues to enable image restoration driven by an understanding of mixed degradations (color casts, haze, and low light). To overcome these limitations, we propose PolUR, a unified multimodal language-driven framework that jointly uses polarization and RGB information to identify degradation types and severity levels through structured semantic instructions and enhances joint restoration representations under the guidance of degradation-aware semantic features to improve restoration robustness under mixed degradations. We further construct a new benchmark named MixPolar, which contains underwater images exhibiting real-world degradations and their corresponding degradation descriptions. MixPolar supports systematic evaluation of degradation perception, image restoration, and performance on downstream tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PolUR effectively integrates polarization and RGB information, achieving competitive performance in degradation perception and image restoration, while also demonstrating generalization capability and utility for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [300] [Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Supervised Framework with Supervised Contrastive Learning for Sugarcane Leaf Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17996)
*Shalini Dangi,Divya Teja,Omkar Kabde,Shahid Shafi Dar,Nagendra Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出U-SSDNet半监督病害检测网络，融合离散小波变换预处理、KL散度伪标签精炼与监督对比学习，在甘蔗叶病害数据集上以极少标注样本逼近全监督性能并优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物病害检测中，传统深度学习模型依赖大规模标注数据，成本高昂且制备困难；同时面临三类挑战：(i) 因生长阶段症状差异导致的高类内变异，(ii) 病健组织间细微的类间相似性，(iii) 噪声标签损害模型可靠性。因此需要能在利用无标注数据的同时保持鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知均值教师半监督学习病害检测网络U-SSDNet。三部分构成：一是离散小波变换预处理，增强病斑可见性并降低类内变异；二是利用KL散度精炼伪标签，缓解确认偏误的影响；三是引入监督对比学习，使特征更具判别性、提升类间可分性。

Result: 在甘蔗叶病害数据集（作为代表性案例）上的实验表明，该方法在仅需远少于全监督的标注样本条件下，性能接近全监督模型，并持续优于现有最先进的监督与半监督方法。

Conclusion: U-SSDNet通过不确定性感知的均值教师框架结合小波预处理、伪标签精炼与对比学习，有效缓解类内变异、类间相似和标签噪声问题，为低标注成本下的作物病害检测提供了可行的半监督方案。

Abstract: Sugarcane leaf diseases severely impact crop yield and quality, making accurate and timely detection critical for sustainable agriculture. Conventional deep learning models for plant disease detection depend on large annotated datasets, which are expensive to obtain and challenging to prepare due to challenges commonly observed across crop disease detection tasks, including (i) high intra-class variation caused by diverse symptom expressions across growth stages, (ii) subtle inter-class similarities between diseased and healthy tissues, and (iii) the risk of noisy labels degrading model reliability. To handle these challenges, we presented an Uncertainty-aware Mean-Teacher based Semi-Supervised Learning Disease Detection Network (U-SSDNet) that leverages unlabeled data while maintaining robustness. First, discrete wavelet transform preprocessing is applied to enhance lesion visibility and reduce intra- class variation. Subsequently, the KL divergence is employed to refine the pseudo-labels and reduce the impact of confirmation bias. At the same time, supervised contrastive learning is applied to make the features more distin- guishable and improve the separation among different classes. Experimental evaluations on sugarcane leaf disease datasets, considered a representative case study, demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves performance close to that of fully supervised models while requiring far fewer annotated samples and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and semi-supervised approaches.

</details>


### [301] [Surface Reconstruction from Range Scans Under Visibility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18005)
*Gabriel Lenain,Edmond Boyer,Jean Ponce*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将表面重建建模为沿每个采样射线的稳健回归问题，把表面表示为1-Lipschitz MLP的零集，用球体追踪求首次相交并用Huber损失回归，无需定向法向、采样离面点或Eikonal损失，在含噪与离群数据上比现有方法更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离扫描的表面重建在噪声与离群点存在时很难：既要拟合观测，又要对测量误差保持鲁棒。现有神经方法大多丢弃传感器位置与可见性信息，只能靠几何先验或正则项来弥补，导致在数据受污染时保真度和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将表面重建表述为稳健回归：沿每条采集射线预测首次表面交点，并将其与实测深度作回归，同时对传感器与观测点之间线段内的预测交点施加惩罚。表面隐式表示为1-Lipschitz多层感知机的零集，从而可用线性收敛的球体追踪计算首次命中，回归采用稳健的Huber损失。

Result: 在合成与真实数据、不同噪声和离群污染水平下与最先进方法比较：干净数据上性能相当，而在即便中等的污染下保真度与鲁棒性大幅提升。分析还证实了保真度与鲁棒性之间的权衡：拟合能力越强，干净情形下保真度越高，但对真实采集固有测量误差越敏感，通常在中等容量处出现最佳折中。
